# Minecraft pack handbook

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

Find the system you want to investigate, check what is known for this version, then follow its guide. This handbook does not establish or impose a shared technology-to-magic or character-progression order. Starting and awarding paths remain partly unmapped.

## Start here
- [Getting started](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/getting-started/): recipes, controls and choosing your first system.
- [Pack map](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/pack-map/): what is installed and what still needs investigation.
- [Mod reference](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/mods/): exact versions and disabled mods.
- [Troubleshooting](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/troubleshooting/): symptoms, saved settings and limits.

## First guides
- [Find a recipe or an item's uses](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/).
- [Prepare your first AE2 storage system](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ae2-starter/).
- [Make your first MI bronze](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/mi-starter/).

These are saved-settings or shipped-documentation guides. They have not been gameplay-tested as complete tutorials in this pack. Awakening, reincarnation, evolution and dimension-access instructions are not ready yet.

## Ask your own LLM
Give your model the handbook link and the specific question. Ask it to check the baseline and cite the page it used. If it cannot open links or follow the index, attach the combined text edition linked in the site navigation. Model access, context limits and answers vary, so a link alone is not a guarantee.

Suggested request:

```text
Use this handbook for my Minecraft pack. Check its baseline and evidence status before answering. Cite the relevant page. Do not fill missing progression steps with guesses from other versions. If the answer is not documented, tell me what is missing.
```

## What this edition proves
Installed files and selected saved settings were inspected. Every active archive was indexed, but only nine archives received representative gameplay-data reading. This is not a full mechanics reference or a compatibility certificate.

[Sources and evidence](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/sources/) explain the limits. [Changes](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/changes/) records the snapshot.


## Candidate snapshot scope

This is a proposed documentation snapshot, not an applied wiki release or a tested pack release.

Four named archives were freshly hashed and their shipped metadata/text inspected: Dungeons and Taverns, Iron's Spellbooks, Tensura and Twilight Forest. Their declared mod identities and dependency tables match the retained earlier declarations; their archive hashes differ. This does not establish why they differ or that their gameplay is unchanged.

The remaining roster identities are imported from the earlier snapshot. A separate named-file hash audit matched the other 118 existing archive filenames, but did not enumerate newly added filenames. The roster counts—120 active archives, two disabled archives and 121 active declared IDs—describe this candidate roster, not a fresh complete installed-population audit.

The snapshot timestamp records the four-archive documentation observation. It is not a new observation of saved settings, effective server/world rules, tag resolution, recipe overrides or gameplay. Saved-setting descriptions remain historical evidence unless explicitly refreshed. No game launch, recipe craft, progression award, persistence or compatibility test is implied.

Only this sanitized roster is a public-baseline candidate. Private witnesses, impact ledgers, local paths, raw archive content and lifecycle receipts must remain outside the public wiki. Guides retain claim-specific evidence and unknowns; a new snapshot label alone does not validate their instructions.

# Pack map

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

## Choose a direction
| System | Installed examples | Start here / current limit |
|---|---|---|
| Machines and processing | Modern Industrialization | Forge Hammer to bronze is documented; full steam automation is not yet mapped. |
| Searchable storage | Applied Energistics 2 | Meteorite materials and the small network are documented; MI power compatibility is Unknown. |
| Portable and bulk storage | Sophisticated Backpacks, Storage Drawers | Installed; upgrade rules and capacities still need mapping. |
| Spellcraft and rituals | Ars Nouveau, Iron's Spells 'n Spellbooks, Occultism, Nature's Aura | [Ars](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ars-first-spell/), [Iron's](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/iron-first-scroll/), [Occultism](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/occultism-first-workbench/) and [Nature's Aura](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/natures-aura-first-garden/) have independent bounded starters; advanced curricula and effective gameplay remain incomplete. |
| Character progression | Tensura, SoloCraft: Reawakening, Tensura-Leveling | Installed; awakening, reincarnation and evolution entry paths are Unknown here. |
| Bosses and dimensions | Twilight Forest, Bosses of Mass Destruction, Tensura boss addons | Installed; encounters, entry/exit and death recovery are not tutorial-ready. |
| Exploration | Dungeons and Taverns, YUNG's Better Dungeons, Tectonic | Shipped resources indexed; this does not prove encounter placement. |
| Loot and equipment | Lootr, Artifacts, Relics, Reliquified Twilight Forest | Installed; acquisition and levelling need further checks. |
| Farming and building | Farmer's Delight, Chipped, Supplementaries | [Cooking pot](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-cooking-pot/), [rice/cutting](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-rice-and-cutting/), [Chipped](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-chipped-workstations/) and [Supplementaries](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-supplementaries-devices/) have bounded guides; broader farming/building loops remain incomplete. |
| Everyday tools | EMI/JEI, Jade, IPN, Xaero, FTB Chunks/Teams | Selected saved settings mapped; key contexts and multiplayer policies need testing. |

For an independent rest project, read [Comforts rest](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-comforts-rest/): rest is not a new respawn guarantee. These links do not certify completed curricula or gameplay.

## Connections we can and cannot claim
- Reliquified Twilight Forest ships recipes referencing Twilight Forest items. That is concrete cross-mod recipe data, not a successful craft or a tested progression shortcut.
- AE2 and MI are both installed. Their power/automation interface has not been established by this mapping, so do not assume their cables or energy units connect directly.
- Tensura-Leveling and SoloCraft have separate declared IDs. Similar names do not prove shared levels, skills or awakening.
- An advancement's parent can position it in the UI. It does not automatically impose a gameplay gate. Some criteria do contain explicit checks; those need to be read individually.

## Coverage
All **120 active top-level JARs** are indexed: nine have representative gameplay data read, 93 have resource-path classification only, and 18 are metadata-only/unmapped. The **121 declared top-level mod IDs** are a different count. Two JARs are disabled. Nested libraries are not additional active top-level mods.

The active archives contain 9,950 recipe JSON entries, 4,498 advancement JSON entries and 2,626 loot-table JSON entries. These are shipped files, not final runtime IDs or working recipes. Conditions, tags, overrides, generated data and server settings can change what is available.

## Customization boundary
The saved enabled resource-pack list includes an icon pack and local client-resource repair packs. Static inspection found assets rather than server data in those selected ZIPs. This does not prove a live reload or a repaired gameplay feature. Global Moonlight datapack and coremods directories were empty when inspected. Generated resource providers, final override precedence and world/server configuration remain unresolved.

## Questions still open
- How is SoloCraft awakening awarded, and what does Tensura reincarnation/evolution require?
- Which documented magic starter steps resolve under effective recipes/settings, and which advanced paths still need evidence?
- How do dimension entry, exit and death recovery work in this exact build?
- Which key overlaps are harmless contexts versus real conflicts?
- Which claims, team permissions and force-loading settings are enforced in multiplayer?

[First guides](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/getting-started/) cover only the evidence-backed starting points. [Sources](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/sources/) explains the status labels.

# Getting started

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

## Before you start
Use the same pack revision as your friends. This handbook describes a developing local baseline, not a released server pack or a tested import download. Do not update individual mods just to match an online tutorial.

## Find things before memorising recipes
Open an inventory screen and try [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/). The saved EMI layout places search on the right, with favourites on the left. Exact recipe ingredients and recipe-viewer categories still need runtime checks, so this handbook does not replace the current in-game viewer.

## Choose your first project
- Want searchable storage? Read [AE2 starter preparation](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ae2-starter/).
- Want machines? Read [MI Forge Hammer and bronze](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/mi-starter/).
- Want magic? Choose independently: [Ars first spell](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ars-first-spell/), [Iron's first scroll](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/iron-first-scroll/), [Occultism first workbench](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/occultism-first-workbench/), or [Nature's Aura first garden](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/natures-aura-first-garden/). Read each evidence status; these are bounded routes, not complete or runtime-accepted curricula.
- Want a homestead project? Choose [Comforts rest](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-comforts-rest/), [cooking pot](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-cooking-pot/), [rice and cutting](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-rice-and-cutting/), [Chipped workstations](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-chipped-workstations/), or [Supplementaries devices](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/homestead-supplementaries-devices/). Check each prerequisite and stop condition; technology is not a prerequisite.
- Want Tensura or SoloCraft? Use the [character route selector](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/character-start/); first access and awakening/award continuity remain unresolved. The [pack map](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/pack-map/) retains the wider scope.

## Saved controls to check
| Action | Saved binding | Limit |
|---|---|---|
| EMI search | Ctrl+F | Inventory/recipe context; JEI also has overlapping actions. |
| Recipes / uses | R / U | Item context; not a global command. |
| Xaero minimap settings | Y | Saved, not demonstrated in this edition. |
| New Xaero waypoint | B | No personal waypoints are included. |
| Map | M | Both Xaero and FTB Chunks map bindings use M; resolve in Controls if necessary. |

No key overlap is called a confirmed bug without testing. The saved FTB minimap is off; that does not disable claims or protection. Inventory Profiles Next has its overlay/editor/settings buttons hidden, so the lack of those buttons is expected saved UI state.

## Keep comparisons consistent
Saved settings are GUI scale 2, render distance 12, simulation distance 6, VSync off and a 120 FPS cap. Memory override is 1024–8192 MiB. These are observed settings, not minimum hardware requirements or a performance guarantee. Leave someone else's instance unchanged unless they want a setting changed.

[Display and recipe troubleshooting](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/troubleshooting/) lists what to check without changing the mod stack.
--- END PAGE: Getting started ---

# Troubleshooting

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

## Recipe search is missing or in the wrong place
The saved EMI search sidebar is right and Center Search Bar is off. Ctrl+O toggles EMI visibility in its context. JEI also has overlapping bindings, so verify what actually responds before changing mods. A missing category or transfer is not proof that the recipe itself is broken.

JEI's optional synthetic tag-recipe browsing is saved off. Normal JEI integrations and EMI recipe import have not been exhaustively tested by this mapping. Do not describe all JEI features as disabled.

## Map and inventory controls overlap
M is saved for both Xaero's map and FTB Chunks' map. C, Z and right bracket also have contextual overlaps among installed features. Check the relevant Controls entries and the screen you are in. The overlap alone does not establish a broken binding.

## Shadows are missing
Iris is saved enabled with MakeUp Ultra Fast 9.5g selected, but the shader override explicitly saves shadow casting off. That explains the saved intent, not a live rendering result. Older notes about keeping shadows do not override the current saved value. No setting has been silently corrected by this handbook.

## An optimiser appears disabled
AE2 Crafting Optimizer has its compiled graph and authoritative planner saved off, while other optimisation domains and execution features remain on. That is not an all-off state. Saved switches do not prove compatibility, active runtime use or a speed gain.

## Claims or force-loading differ from this handbook
FTB global files contain templates and defaults. Existing worlds, teams, permissions or server configuration may override them. This edition does not establish effective quotas, protection rules or operator access. Do not rely on a claim as tested protection of valuables until the server policy is checked.

## Terrain or distant rendering behaves strangely
Distant Horizons is installed. Saved generation mode is FEATURES with SURFACE_THEN_CHUNKS, eight threads and runtime ratio 1.0. Those are observations, not a recommended fix or a hard CPU cap. Rendering distant terrain does not keep distant machines simulated.

Do not transplant a separate trial's configuration or patch into this baseline. A static inspection or menu startup is not proof that generation, boss encounters, reloads or save/reopen work correctly.

## Reporting a problem
Give the pack revision, relevant mod/system, action, expected result and what happened. Redact account data, addresses and world locations before sharing any diagnostic material. This wiki does not collect logs or private saves.

[Sources and evidence](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/sources/) explain what is verified and what remains Unknown.
--- END PAGE: Troubleshooting ---

# Sources and evidence

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

## Status labels
| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Installed | Exact top-level archive bytes were found and fingerprinted. Not proof they loaded successfully. |
| Saved configuration | A selected global setting was read. Not proof of effective runtime/server state. |
| Shipped documentation/data | The exact installed archive contains the cited text or resource. Conditions/overrides may still change gameplay. |
| Runtime tested | Reserved for an explicitly described exercise in the exact pack. The starter tutorials in this edition do not have this status. |
| Unknown | Evidence is missing or a mechanism remains unresolved. Do not replace it with a guess. |

## Baseline and reproducibility
Baseline ID: `dev-0.5-20261015-r2`. The UTC inventory was captured late on 14 October; the snapshot ID uses its 15 October Singapore date. Exact versions and SHA-256 identities are retained in the safe baseline payload. Public content does not include local absolute paths, raw configs, logs, saves or mod binaries.

Archive citations use `archive.jar!internal/path`. They point inside the installed version, not to a claim about the newest upstream release. They are not downloadable JAR links and do not grant redistribution rights.

The first map independently rechecked all 120 active archive hashes, 112 sampled data/text records and 19 selected-settings source hashes. It did not test a game session, final recipe registry, multiplayer compatibility, performance or complete progression. A snapshot can become stale as development continues.

## Where the first guides come from
- AE2 19.2.18: its shipped `assets/ae2/ae2guide/getting-started.md` and selected charger/inscriber recipe data.
- Modern Industrialization 2.5.10: its shipped Forge Hammer and first-bronze guide pages.
- Recipe navigation: saved EMI 1.1.24 configuration, cross-checked against the saved JEI controls context.
- Pack/system map: top-level mod metadata and counted resource paths. Recognised namespaces can include compatibility assets for absent mods, so a namespace alone is not an installed-mod list.

## Attribution
Minecraft belongs to its respective rights holders. Mod names and identifiers belong to their authors. The handbook is an unofficial pack-specific summary, not official support for Minecraft or any included mod. It includes original concise explanations and exact source pointers rather than republishing full mod guidebooks.

## Updating safely
Capture a new private inventory after approved pack changes, compare added/removed/changed archives and selected settings, then review affected pages. Never overwrite curated guides just because a file hash changed. Re-export the text editions after an accepted content change. An unreviewed automated refresh is not part of this edition.

[Changes](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/changes/) records accepted snapshots. [Pack map](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/pack-map/) lists unanswered player questions.
--- END PAGE: Sources and evidence ---

# Changes

## dev-0.5-20261015-r2
Proposed documentation snapshot of the developing Solo–Tensura pack on Minecraft 1.21.1 / NeoForge 21.1.250. Candidate **dev-0.5-20261015-r2 remains unapplied and not canonically adopted**; the earlier canonical documentation baseline remains **dev-0.5-20261015**. Separately, the preceding 66-page r2 integration received bounded local documentary acceptance only; this 69-page cross-cutting preview remains under review. Neither is canonical adoption, runtime acceptance or publication. This does not announce an accepted pack update.

### Four-archive impact routing
Four named archives have different hashes from the retained earlier declarations, while declared identities and dependency tables match. # Find recipes and item uses

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

## Baseline
EMI 1.1.24 and JEI 19.32.0.359 in `dev-0.5-20261015-r2`.

## Evidence status
**Saved configuration.** These bindings/layout values were read from global files. Recipe categories, contextual key handling and transfers have not been exercised as part of this documentation check.

## Prerequisites
An open inventory/recipe-viewer context with EMI enabled. If the sidebar is hidden, check its saved visibility binding rather than reinstalling mods.

## Steps
1. In an inventory screen, look for the item sidebar on the right. Press Ctrl+F to focus EMI search in its supported context.
2. Type an item name. The saved search includes tooltips by default; do not assume tags or mod names are included without a prefix or setting change.
3. Hover an item and try R for recipes, or U for uses. EMI also saves left/right-click recipe/use actions in the appropriate item context.
4. Press A on an item in the applicable context to add it to favourites. The saved favourites panel is on the left.
5. Use Backspace to go back in EMI's supported context.

## Expected result
A matching item list and a recipe/uses view for a supported category. This is the intended UI flow from saved bindings, not a promise that every custom recipe is registered or transferable.

## Common failures
- No sidebar: Ctrl+O is the saved EMI visibility toggle; JEI has an overlapping binding, so test the actual screen response.
- U opens a waypoint-related screen: the same key has contextual uses. Check Controls and the screen you are using.
- An ingredient does not transfer: first check the category, inventory, container and missing ingredients. Do not treat transfer failure as proof the craft is impossible.

## Sources
Saved global `config/emi.css` binding/layout descriptions and selected `config/jei/jei-client.ini` settings. The handbook omits raw personal configuration. [Troubleshooting](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/troubleshooting/) covers overlaps.
--- END PAGE: Find recipes and item uses ---

# Prepare your first AE2 storage system

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

## Baseline
Applied Energistics 2 **19.2.18** in `dev-0.5-20261015-r2`.

## Evidence status
**Shipped documentation/data.** The installed guide supports this route. This is a preparation/build outline, not a gameplay-tested end-to-end network tutorial. A working power source and this pack's cross-mod power compatibility are not established here.

## Prerequisites
Access to meteorite materials, a way to make the listed AE2 components, and a power supply compatible with the energy acceptor. Check each current recipe in [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/). Do not assume MI cables are compatible merely because MI is installed.

## Steps
1. Find an AE2 meteorite. The shipped guide suggests a Meteorite Compass if needed; check its actual recipe before crafting.
2. Collect certus material and the Mysterious Cube's four inscriber presses. **Do not break flawless budding certus with a pickaxe, even Silk Touch**: the guide says it degrades and cannot be restored to flawless.
3. Use the Charger to obtain charged certus as needed. The guide makes Fluix from charged certus, redstone and nether quartz in water.
4. Use the Inscriber and presses to prepare processors. Consult the current recipe view for each processor rather than mixing up printed circuits and completed processors.
5. Prepare one ME Drive, one Terminal or Crafting Terminal, one Energy Acceptor, a few ordinary glass/covered/smart cables and storage cells. The shipped small-network list does not require a controller.
6. Place the cube Energy Acceptor beside the ME Drive, or place the flat Energy Acceptor on a cable attached to the drive, as described in the shipped guide. Connect the terminal through cable, insert the cells, and supply compatible energy. Stop here if the power interface is unresolved rather than adding random power mods.

## Expected result
The shipped guide's target is a small searchable ME inventory, without autocrafting. Whether it works with the chosen power source and current pack configuration still needs a real build check.

## Common failures
- Breaking flawless budding certus loses its flawless form according to the installed guide. Preserve the source in place.
- A storage cell outside a drive/chest is not the network storage setup described here.
- A dark terminal can have several causes; this handbook has not validated your energy, channels or connections. Do not infer a missing controller as the default cause.
- A later network expansion needs a separate channels/devices check; this guide does not certify a large network or optimiser correctness.

## Sources
- `appliedenergistics2-19.2.18.jar!assets/ae2/ae2guide/getting-started.md`
- `appliedenergistics2-19.2.18.jar!data/ae2/recipe/charger/guide.json`
- `appliedenergistics2-19.2.18.jar!data/ae2/recipe/inscriber/calculation_processor_print.json`
- `appliedenergistics2-19.2.18.jar!data/ae2/recipe/inscriber/calculation_processor.json`

[Pack map](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/pack-map/) separates this storage route from unverified cross-mod automation.
--- END PAGE: Prepare your first AE2 storage system ---

# Make your first MI bronze

> Development snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. The pack is still changing.

## Baseline
Modern Industrialization **2.5.10** in `dev-0.5-20261015-r2`.

## Evidence status
**Shipped documentation.** The installed Forge Hammer and bronze pages support this sequence. Effective recipes, tag substitutions and machine operation have not been tested in-game for this handbook.

## Prerequisites
Ingredients for the Forge Hammer and access to copper/tin materials. Use [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/) for exact recipes. This page deliberately does not invent a copper-to-tin ratio or a complete first-boiler build.

## Steps
1. Find the Forge Hammer recipe in the current viewer and gather its ingredients. The installed guide puts it before bronze processing.
2. Make the Forge Hammer, then use it to process copper/tin ingots into dusts, or nuggets into tiny dusts, following the current recipe display.
3. Combine the matching copper and tin dusts into bronze dust. Keep dust and tiny-dust recipes separate; the guide states that nine tiny dusts make one dust.
4. Smelt bronze dust to get bronze ingots.
5. Before moving to steam automation, read the next shipped guide pages and verify the boiler, pipes, fuel/water and machine recipes. That full operational setup is not yet mapped here.

## Expected result
Bronze ingots for the next MI stage, if the current recipes and processing UI match the shipped guide. The guide also describes Forge Hammer ore processing and a hand-held Steam Mining Drill, but those are separate from a validated steam-machine network.

## Common failures
- Similar dusts from different mods may not substitute unless the actual recipe tags/unification allow it. Check the viewer rather than assuming.
- Dust and tiny dust are different quantities. Do not use the same ingredient count blindly.
- The hand-held Steam Mining Drill needs both water and solid fuel according to the installed guide. That does not establish how a boiler or steam pipe is operated.
- No MI-to-AE2 power bridge is verified by this starter page.

## Sources
- `Modern-Industrialization-2.5.10.jar!assets/modern_industrialization/mi_guidebook/steam_age/forge_hammer.md`
- `Modern-Industrialization-2.5.10.jar!assets/modern_industrialization/mi_guidebook/steam_age/making_bronze.md`

[Pack map](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/pack-map/) lists the next systems without imposing a shared progression gate.
--- END PAGE: Make your first MI bronze ---

# Choose a storage direction

## Baseline
Development snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**, Minecraft 1.21.1 / NeoForge 21.1.250. AE2 19.2.18, Storage Drawers 13.11.4 and Sophisticated Backpacks 3.26.9 are installed.

## Evidence status
Shipped guide, translations and selected recipes were read. No storage interaction, transfer or persistence test was run. This hub helps you choose a route; it does not certify a working combined network.

## Prerequisites
Decide whether your immediate problem is carrying loot, organizing workshop materials or searching a base inventory. Use [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/) to check the chosen item's current recipe. Those saved bindings are not a guarantee of custom-category coverage.

## Steps
1. If you need a portable option, examine the Backpack candidate in [choose your first storage route](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/storage-choice/). Its shipped recipe uses leather, string and a wooden-chest tag, with an item-enabled condition.
2. If you want workshop compartments, examine the oak 1x1 drawer recipe in the same guide. Do not infer effective capacity from the item's name.
3. If your goal is a searchable ME inventory, follow [AE2 preparation](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ae2-starter/), which separates materials, processors and the small network. Compatible power is still a blocker, not an assumed MI prerequisite.
4. Before joining systems, read [storage boundaries](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/storage-boundaries/). Treat each as a separate route until its interface has evidence.

## Expected result
A chosen first route and a short list of unresolved prerequisites. You have not yet proved a successful craft or safe migration of existing stock.

## Common failures
- Planning a searchable inventory does not provide power. Stop at the AE2 power boundary rather than crafting a controller as a universal fix.
- A backpack's filter and void upgrades are different tools: the shipped text describes pipe filtering versus item deletion. Keep voiding out of your initial route. See the [deletion boundary](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/storage-boundaries/#deletion-is-not-capacity).
- Do not make this route a mandatory gate for magic or exploration; no such pack-wide ordering is established.

## Sources
- `appliedenergistics2-19.2.18.jar!assets/ae2/ae2guide/getting-started.md`
- `StorageDrawers-neoforge-1.21.1-13.11.4.jar!data/storagedrawers/recipe/oak_full_drawers_1.json`
- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!data/sophisticatedbackpacks/recipe/backpack.json`
- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!assets/sophisticatedbackpacks/lang/en_us.json`
--- END PAGE: Choose a storage direction ---

# Choose your first storage route

## Baseline
Development snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**: Storage Drawers 13.11.4, Sophisticated Backpacks 3.26.9 and AE2 19.2.18.

## Evidence status
Static decision how-to, not a tested crafting or migration tutorial. The recipes below are shipped declarations; enabled conditions, resolved tags, overrides and recipe-viewer coverage still need confirmation.

## Prerequisites
A clear goal and access to [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/). Do not move valuable stock or install deletion upgrades as part of choosing a route. The [storage hub](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/systems/storage/) keeps the alternatives separate.

## Steps
1. Write down one immediate outcome: carry items, use workshop compartments, or search an ME inventory. Choose only the matching route below; these are alternatives, not a progression sequence.
2. For carrying items, look up `sophisticatedbackpacks:backpack`. Its shipped pattern is `SLS / SCS / LLL`: S is the string tag, L the leather tag, C the wooden-chest tag. The declared output is one Backpack. The recipe uses the custom `sophisticatedbackpacks:basic_backpack` type and an item-enabled condition; if the viewer has no entry, stop rather than assume the pattern is currently craftable.
3. For workshop compartments, look up `storagedrawers:oak_full_drawers_1`. The shipped pattern has three oak planks across the top and bottom rows and a wooden-chest-tag ingredient in the middle slot. It declares one drawer. Keep capacities, locks and item removal as unresolved interaction checks, not guessed instructions.
4. For searchable storage, use [AE2 preparation](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ae2-starter/). Its material and processor route is documented, but working power must be established before you treat it as usable storage. MI installation alone does not close that prerequisite.
5. Record the chosen item, the recipe you actually see and the first unresolved boundary. Use [storage boundaries](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/storage-boundaries/) before any later transfer or upgrade work.

## Expected result
You can name the route and its next prerequisite without buying into an unverified cross-system dependency. This how-to completes a choice, not a working in-game build.

## Common failures
- Missing backpack recipe: its enabled condition and custom serializer need checking. Do not remove mods or rewrite configs to force the declaration to load.
- Conflicting output: stop before taking the result. Polymorph is installed, but this pilot has not established its exact controls or supported containers. Record the displayed output ID and context; do not repeatedly consume ingredients.
- No recipe transfer: check the current item/container/category and ingredients as described in [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/#common-failures); failed transfer does not prove a recipe impossible.

## Tips with trade-offs
- Before preparing an AE2 expansion, preserve flawless budding certus in place. The shipped guide says a pickaxe degrades it even with Silk Touch, with no repair to flawless. Benefit: keep the non-degrading source; trade-off: travel to it until a separately evidenced relocation route is available. This is resource preservation, not an instruction to attempt advanced transport.
- When selecting backpack automation, start by investigating the Filter Upgrade rather than a Void Upgrade. The translation describes filtering piped input/output, while voiding deletes selected items. Benefit: frame a non-deletion design; prerequisite: verify the actual filter UI and pipe compatibility separately. Trade-off: rejected items still need an overflow plan, which is not yet verified here.

## Sources
- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!data/sophisticatedbackpacks/recipe/backpack.json`
- `StorageDrawers-neoforge-1.21.1-13.11.4.jar!data/storagedrawers/recipe/oak_full_drawers_1.json`
- `appliedenergistics2-19.2.18.jar!assets/ae2/ae2guide/getting-started.md`
- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!assets/sophisticatedbackpacks/lang/en_us.json`

# Storage boundaries and unresolved interfaces

## Baseline
**dev-0.5-20261015-r2**, Minecraft 1.21.1 / NeoForge 21.1.250. Exact archive versions appear in the sources below.

## Evidence status
Static reference. A translated feature description establishes shipped intent, not active configuration, tested compatibility or item persistence.

## Prerequisites
Use [storage choice](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/storage-choice/) to select an independent route before planning integration.

## Steps
For a proposed connection, identify the producing system, receiving system, item/fluid/energy type, direction, filter and overflow behavior. Leave any unproven field unknown. Do not move your main inventory to answer an unknown.

## Expected result
A boundary checklist that distinguishes a known declaration from an untested handoff.

| Boundary | Evidence available | Not established |
|---|---|---|
| Drawer starter | `storagedrawers:oak_full_drawers_1` recipe declares oak planks and wooden-chest tag | Capacity, click controls, locking persistence and controller range |
| Backpack starter | `sophisticatedbackpacks:backpack` custom recipe and enabled condition; [first backpack access](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/first-backpack-access/) supplies provisional same-version upstream held-item use evidence | Exact installed open control and build-2195 correspondence remain unknown; effective recipe, slots, Curios behavior, deposit/withdrawal, carrying lifecycle and contents through upgrades/persistence are not established |
| ME starter | Shipped guide describes drive, cells, terminal, cables and energy acceptor; small route does not require controller | Chosen power source, effective channels and working network |
| MI -> AE2 | Both identities are installed | Energy units/interface conversion, sided item/fluid transfers and automation safety |
| Drawers/backpack -> ME | Candidate connection only | Interface choice, priorities, looping, filtering and overflow behavior |

The access guide separates held-item Use Item from placement; it is not proof of the installed compiled behavior or an established open key. Stop if the interface differs and keep the empty Backpack; do not infer safe storage from an empty menu. J2's processing/interface handoff remains unresolved.

## Deletion is not capacity
Storage Drawers' shipped Void Upgrade description says it destroys excess items. Sophisticated Backpacks' Void Upgrade description says it deletes items selected by a filter; its recipe also carries an item-enabled condition. Neither is ordinary extra capacity. Avoid both while learning storage. If deletion is suspected, stop feeding the affected container and record the upgrade/filter context before further interaction; this reference cannot promise an undo or restore deleted items.

## Common failures
- A dark ME terminal is not evidence that you need a controller. Return to [AE2's unresolved power and connection checks](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ae2-starter/#common-failures).
- Equal ingredient tags do not establish identical effective outputs or interchangeable machine interfaces.
- No test here establishes save/reopen persistence. Keep a later authorized check limited to expendable items and an isolated world; do not test by breaking a stocked container.

## Sources
- `appliedenergistics2-19.2.18.jar!assets/ae2/ae2guide/getting-started.md`
- `StorageDrawers-neoforge-1.21.1-13.11.4.jar!data/storagedrawers/recipe/oak_full_drawers_1.json`
- `StorageDrawers-neoforge-1.21.1-13.11.4.jar!assets/storagedrawers/lang/en_us.json`
- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!data/sophisticatedbackpacks/recipe/backpack.json`
- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!data/sophisticatedbackpacks/recipe/void_upgrade.json`
- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!assets/sophisticatedbackpacks/lang/en_us.json`

# Find the control for the screen you are using

## Baseline
Minecraft 1.21.1, NeoForge 21.1.250; Controlling 19.0.5, EMI 1.1.24 and JEI 19.32.0.359. How-to: identify a control before changing it.

## Evidence status
Saved bindings and exact-version UI labels; **not runtime tested**. The same physical key can serve different screens. A duplicate binding is a lead, not proof of a conflict. This guide does not prescribe a replacement key.

## Prerequisites
Know the screen where the unwanted action occurs and the action you intended. Start with no item on the cursor. Do not investigate by pressing deletion, drop or bulk-transfer controls while carrying valuable items.

## Steps
1. Write down the context: looking at the world, ordinary inventory, a recipe page or a particular container. Include what is hovered and whether a search field has focus. These distinctions matter more than a key name alone.
2. Open the game's controls list. Controlling supplies **Show All**, **Show Conflicts**, **Show Unbound** and **Available Keys**. Use Show All to find the intended action, then Show Conflicts to inspect possible overlaps. Show Unbound means an action needs a binding; it does not mean the feature is absent.
3. For [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/), compare the saved actions below. Start with Ctrl+F and a plain item-name query, not a crafting click. The expected checkpoint is a focused search field and matching items.
4. If U opens another screen, compare the saved JEI/EMI uses action with the waypoint action in the table. Return to the inventory/recipe context; do not conclude that the recipe viewer is broken merely because a world-context action won.
5. If a binding must change, record its original value first and change only the intended action. Available Keys is a candidate list, not a guarantee against every mod-specific mouse or contextual handler. Check the same original screen and hovered target with disposable items before trusting the change. Restore the original binding if the intended action still fails. Do not use a global reset to fix one overlap.

## Expected result
You can name the intended action, its saved binding if captured, and the context in which you need it. Runtime success remains an owner check, not a result of this documentation work.

## Common failures
- **No separate JEI panel:** the captured JEI setting is `enabled=false`, whereas EMI saves `enabled=true`. Treat EMI as the first visible route; neither value proves live loading or complete imported-category coverage.
- **Ctrl+O appears to do nothing:** both viewers save the same visibility key. Check which viewer is visible before changing both.
- **A mouse action moves items instead of browsing:** left/right clicks also have inventory meanings. Use R/U over the intended viewer item first, keeping the cursor empty.
- **An action is missing from this table:** the processed settings did not capture every mod's keys. Find the exact action label in Controls or that mod's configuration; do not apply a remembered default. For inventory operations use [item safety](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/inventory-safety/); for world overlays use [Jade identification](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/jade-identification/).

## Saved binding reference
| Action | Captured binding | Context/limit |
|---|---|---|
| Open inventory | E | Saved game control |
| EMI focus search / JEI focus search | Ctrl+F / Ctrl+F | Supported viewer context; field focus must be visible |
| EMI recipes / uses | R or left click / U or right click | Viewer item context, not a general inventory gesture |
| EMI favourites / back | A / Backspace | Applicable viewer context |
| EMI visibility / JEI overlay | Ctrl+O / Ctrl+O | Potential overlap, not reproduced conflict |
| EMI craft one / craft all | Left click / Shift+left click | Crafting action context; avoid bulk action until output is checked |
| JEI recipe back / close | Backspace / Escape | JEI recipe GUI, if accessible |
| JEI previous / next recipe page | Page Up / Page Down | JEI recipe GUI |
| JEI previous / next category | Shift+Page Up / Shift+Page Down | JEI recipe GUI |
| Xaero waypoint screen | U | Saved overlap with item uses; separate system, not a recipe function |

`key.keyboard.unknown` denotes an unbound captured entry, not a physical key called Unknown. Uncaptured IPN, Jade, TrashSlot and Crafting Tweaks bindings are **unknown**, not unbound.

## Sources
Processed saved-controls evidence and global EMI/JEI settings for this baseline; Controlling 19.0.5 `assets/controlling/lang/en_us.json` (Show Conflicts, Show Unbound, Available Keys). Private retained witnesses and claim IDs E-CONTROL, E-VIEWER, E-IPN-SAVED are provided to reviewers. No original settings were reopened.

<!-- wiki.py managed raw export schema=1 -->
Source: https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/inventory-safety/
Baseline: dev-0.5-20261015-r2
Spoiler: safe

# Organize items without treating locks as a backup

## Baseline
Inventory Profiles Next 2.2.5, Mouse Tweaks 2.26.1, Crafting Tweaks 21.1.11 and TrashSlot 21.1.11. How-to: prepare a bounded inventory operation and avoid destructive shortcuts.

## Evidence status
Exact shipped option descriptions plus processed saved IPN settings; **not runtime tested**. Feature descriptions establish what options intend to control, not which options are enabled here. Mouse Tweaks gestures and wheel direction remain unverified for the saved pack configuration. No default hotkey is prescribed.

## Prerequisites
A safe place to stop and an ordinary storage container with spare space. Put irreplaceable equipment and unusually named or enchanted items aside manually before experimenting. Use a few disposable items for the first check; do not start with a machine input/output slot or crafting result.

## Steps
1. Identify the intended operation: sort the player's inventory, sort the opened container, move matching items, refill stacks or clear a crafting grid. [Resolve the action and context](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/control-contexts/) before pressing a hotkey. IPN's saved overlay, inventory editor and inventory settings buttons are hidden; their absence is not evidence that sorting is disabled.
2. For sorting or moving, inspect IPN's **Enable Locked Slots** and lock controls in its own settings. The shipped description says locks exclude slots from sort, move all, clean cursor and crafting/refill supply storage. It also explicitly excludes auto-refill monitoring slots from that protection. No saved lock shortcut or active lock state is captured here: stop if you cannot identify the control and its visual state.
3. Check the separate protections relevant to your task. **Disable Shift+Click and Ctrl+Q and Q** describes inventory-screen protections; **Disable (Q) Drop For Non-Stackable Locked Slots In Game** describes a separate world-context protection. **Disable All User Input on Locked Slots** does not stop automated actions, profiles or auto refill. A lock is not a universal deletion barrier, a grave or a backup.
4. Before a move, inspect **Move All Under the Cursor**. Its description distinguishes source-under-cursor behavior from player-to-container behavior. For a focused item-type operation, inspect **Only focused Item type**: the description says it moves all of the selected type even if that type is not already on the other side. For topping up existing stacks only, inspect **Just refill stacks** instead. Confirm the destination has room and inspect the first disposable result before repeating.
5. Before enabling or relying on auto refill, inspect **Use Items From Locked Slots** and **Refill With Any Food**. The latter can choose an enchanted golden apple before ordinary food, according to the shipped description. **Allow refill with harmful foods** is a further condition. Keep valuable food outside the experiment; do not assume a lock or custom name preserves it.
6. For a crafting grid, use the visibly labelled **Clear Grid** action only after making inventory space. Do **not** substitute **Force Clear Grid**: its exact tooltip says it drops items if necessary. Rotation, balance and spread change grid arrangement; after any such action recheck [the actual output](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/conflicting-outputs/) before taking it. Hotkeys, clear-grid overflow behavior and current Crafting Tweaks mode are not captured; use the labelled supported UI or stop.
7. For TrashSlot, first establish the **Lock/Unlock TrashSlot** control and visible lock state using disposable items only. The captured evidence contains no saved key or active deletion options. Hiding the slot is not a universal safety method: **Allow Deletion While TrashSlot is Invisible** exists. Do not use **Delete All Items of Type** for a first check, and do not promise an undo.

## Expected result
A small, inspected operation with valuable items excluded and the chosen source/destination understood. If a control, mode or protection is unknown, leave the items where they are and use deliberate individual clicks instead of guessing a bulk gesture.

## Common failures
- **Locked slots moved or supplied items:** inspect auto-refill exceptions and automated/profile actions before treating this as a broken lock. Do not reproduce with valuable gear.
- **Row/column sort does not fit around locks:** IPN's description requires the remaining unlocked shape to be rectangular. Ordinary sorting is a different operation; do not unlock precious slots just to obtain a rectangular pattern.
- **Closing the screen did not immediately stop sorting:** with interval-between-clicks enabled, IPN has a separate Stop Sorting If Screen Closes option. Its saved value is unknown. Screen close is not a guaranteed cancel.
- **Wheel movement is surprising:** IPN has item scrolling and Mouse Tweaks is also installed. No saved Mouse Tweaks direction or enabled-gesture evidence was captured. Stop scrolling, empty the cursor and return to individual clicks. Installed co-presence does not establish a reproduced conflict or justify a universal wheel direction.
- **TrashSlot is disabled on a server:** its shipped message says the server must have TrashSlot installed. Do not install or change a server as part of this guide.
- **An item is already gone:** stop further deletion, inspect the visible slot without adding another item, and seek the owner's recovery procedure. This evidence does not establish a recoverable buffer, lifetime, persistence or undo control. Creative delete keys are explicitly described as permanent regardless of Instant Deletion.

## Tips with trade-offs
**Top up rather than evacuate a container.** Prerequisite: identify IPN's Just refill stacks modifier and the move direction. Technique: constrain the operation to existing destination stacks. Benefit: avoids introducing new item types to a deliberately arranged inventory. Trade-off: it will not create a new destination stack; no binding or successful pack transfer is verified.

**Keep valuable food out of refill candidates.** Prerequisite: inspect auto-refill choices. Technique: store special food separately before using an uncertain refill setup. Benefit: avoids the shipped any-food preference choosing rare food. Trade-off: that food is not immediately available on the hotbar. This is a precaution, not an assertion that any-food is enabled.

## Sources
IPN 2.2.5 `assets/inventoryprofilesnext/lang/en_us.json` option descriptions; processed IPN global settings. Crafting Tweaks 21.1.11 `assets/craftingtweaks/lang/en_us.json`; TrashSlot 21.1.11 `assets/trashslot/lang/en_us.json`. Mouse Tweaks 2.26.1 metadata establishes installed presence only. Claims E-IPN-LOCK, E-IPN-MOVE, E-IPN-REFILL, E-GRID and E-TRASH.
--- END PAGE: Organize items without treating locks as a backup ---

<!-- wiki.py managed raw export schema=1 -->

Baseline: dev-0.5-20261015-r2

# Check and choose a conflicting crafting output

## Baseline
Polymorph 1.1.0+1.21.1, EMI 1.1.24 and JEI 19.32.0.359. How-to: avoid consuming ingredients for the wrong result.

## Evidence status
Installed Polymorph identity is freshly hash-matched. The UI procedure below is **supplemental upstream documentation**, pinned to the project's 1.21.x branch, not proven to match the exact installed release. No pack-specific conflicting recipe pair, selection packet success or recipe transfer has been runtime verified. Continue only when the described selector and result actually appear.

## Prerequisites
A crafting screen with a visible result; enough ordinary ingredients for a small test and inventory space for the result. Start from [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/) to identify the item you actually want. Do not use EMI's saved Shift+left-click craft-all action until a single output is confirmed. There is no named guaranteed conflict example in this evidence.

## Steps
1. Find the wanted recipe and note its result name, count and item identity where the UI exposes it. Place the ingredients in the relevant crafting grid. Merely locating the desired item in EMI does not establish that the current result slot produces it.
2. Inspect the actual result slot before taking anything. If it matches your goal, no selector intervention is needed. If it differs, leave the ingredients unconsumed.
3. When those ingredients match multiple recipes and a Polymorph button appears above the output slot, press that button. Upstream describes a list of possible results. If the button is missing or its location differs, stop this procedure rather than clicking an unlabelled nearby control.
4. Select the wanted listed result. The checkpoint is the crafting output changing to that item. If the desired item is absent from the list, do not take another item as a workaround; investigate the recipe, workstation and ingredients instead.
5. Take one ordinary result and inspect it. Recheck name and count before repeating or using a bulk craft. Upstream describes remembering the choice while the ingredients do not change, but this pack has not exercised that behavior. Recheck after changing ingredients, reopening a screen or transferring a recipe.
6. To back out before a craft, do not take the result. Remove the ingredients individually into available inventory slots. Avoid Force Clear Grid because its [drop warning](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/inventory-safety/) makes it unsuitable as a universal rollback.

## Expected result
The visible output and the one inspected craft agree with the wanted item, or you stop without consuming the ingredients. These are reader checkpoints; no craft was executed during authoring.

## Common failures
- **No selector:** it may not be a multiple-recipe situation in the effective pack, or the screen/integration may not expose a selector. Static installation does not decide which cause applies. A removed or overridden recipe cannot be restored merely by choosing another output.
- **The wanted result is not listed:** compare the exact recipe and workstation instead of repeatedly choosing the wrong result. Do not assume recipes with similar ingredient names use interchangeable tags.
- **Viewer transfer chose a different output:** inspect the result after transfer and use the selector only if available. Installed EMI/JEI integration classes do not prove successful transfer on this stack.
- **Selection does not change the output:** stop before crafting. Preserve a description of screen, inputs, visible choices and expected item for the owner. Polymorph ships a Networking Failure message, but this work has not observed such a failure.
- **A furnace also appears ambiguous:** upstream documents a selector above furnace output and block-persisted selection. That is a separate, untested route; this guide does not establish fuel timing, existing-output handling or save/reopen behavior. Do not start a valuable smelt to test it.

## Tip: preview, select, then scale
Prerequisite: a visible supported selector and disposable ingredients. Technique: confirm the selected output with one small craft before bulk action. Benefit: limits the ingredients committed to a wrong output. Trade-off: one extra inspection and no protection against subsequent changed inputs. Sources: upstream crafting selector description and saved EMI craft-one/craft-all bindings.

## Sources
[Polymorph upstream crafting and smelting documentation](https://github.com/illusivesoulworks/polymorph/blob/a2dc1e5a91e5694943d84e32caa729cd477098e3/README.md), pinned 1.21.x branch; exact installed metadata and `assets/polymorph/lang/en_us.json`; processed EMI bindings. Claim E-POLY explicitly retains the release-correspondence limitation. No commands were run to enumerate conflicts and no logs were read.
--- END PAGE: Check and choose a conflicting crafting output ---

# Read an enchantment before committing equipment

## Baseline
EMI Enchanting 0.1.2+1.21+neoforge, Enchantment Descriptions 21.1.11 and EMI 1.1.24. How-to with a compact named-enchantment reference.

## Evidence status
Exact-version translation labels and shipped descriptions; saved EMI lookup bindings. **Not runtime tested.** The Enchantment Info category's presence in a running viewer, generated values, modded-enchantment support and compatibility lists remain unverified. Descriptions are summaries, not damage formulas or promises about pack-specific overrides.

## Prerequisites
An item or enchantment name to inspect; a supported tooltip/recipe-viewer context. Do not equip cursed gear, spend XP or apply an enchantment merely to discover its effect.

## Steps
1. Hover the relevant item or enchanted book and read the enchantment name. Enchantment Descriptions ships the prompt **Hold shift to view enchantment descriptions**. If that prompt is shown, hold Shift while keeping the same target hovered. Shift is a shipped instruction, not proof that this pack's tooltip activation setting is unchanged.
2. Use [EMI search and recipe lookup](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/) to find the named item/book; R is the saved recipes action in the applicable context. If an **Enchantment Info** category appears, open it. That is the exact shipped category label; the evidence does not establish a separate Enchantment Info hotkey or guarantee that R reaches it for every target.
3. In that category, read **Enchantment Description**, **Max Level**, **Valid Items** and **Exclusions** before considering an application. Also distinguish **Treasure**, **Curse**, **Villagers Trade** and **Randomly Selectable**. A category label with a yes/no template is not itself an evaluated value for a particular enchantment.
4. Compare a familiar named description below with the tooltip as a low-risk sanity check. If the UI displays **Unknown Enchantment**, a translation key or no description, record the name and stop making effect/compatibility assumptions. Another mod's namespace in Enchantment Descriptions' bundled language file is not evidence that that mod is installed.
5. Leave equipment unchanged until both the effect and the target-item/exclusion information are understood. If no applicable compatibility information appears, ask for exact-item verification rather than treating a missing exclusion as permission to combine enchantments.

## Expected result
You can distinguish effect text from eligible-item and exclusion information, and identify an unresolved entry without spending resources. This authoring has not applied or combined an enchantment.

## Named description reference
The following are concise original summaries of Enchantment Descriptions' shipped text, not numeric or progression guidance.

| Enchantment ID | Shipped-description meaning | Decision it helps |
|---|---|---|
| `minecraft:mending` | XP repairs armor/tool durability | Do not confuse repair with a slower durability-loss rate |
| `minecraft:unbreaking` | Slows durability loss | Different function from Mending; no stacking compatibility proven here |
| `minecraft:fortune` | Some blocks, such as coal/diamond ore, may drop more items | Not a promise of extra drops from every block |
| `minecraft:silk_touch` | Collects fragile blocks such as glass | Identify collection behavior rather than assuming a yield multiplier |
| `minecraft:infinity` | Free normal-arrow shots require at least one arrow | Do not discard the last normal arrow based on the name alone |
| `minecraft:binding_curse` | Prevents removal from an armor slot | Inspect before equipping; no removal/recovery method established here |
| `minecraft:vanishing_curse` | Destroys the cursed item on death while in inventory | Do not assume a storage or death-recovery mod overrides the curse |
| `minecraft:density` | Attack damage increases with fall distance | Not a fixed damage bonus or tested combat recommendation |
| `minecraft:breach` | Attacks are more effective against armored targets | Distinguish armor-related effect from Density's fall condition |
| `minecraft:wind_burst` | Smash attacks launch the user back into the air | Recognize movement consequences before applying; no safe-height guarantee |

## Common failures
- **Shift does not reveal text:** the shipped prompt is not captured effective configuration. Check the target and visible instruction; do not invent another activation key.
- **No Enchantment Info category:** verify the viewer context and item first. Static addon installation does not certify category registration, modded support or transfer.
- **A book describes an effect but cannot apply:** description availability and Valid Items/Exclusions answer different questions. Do not consume another book to test compatibility.
- **A modded enchantment is unknown:** record its literal ID when available. These vanilla descriptions are not substitutes for that enchantment's own mechanics.

## Tip: compare purpose before level
Prerequisite: readable descriptions. Technique: compare Mending with Unbreaking, or Fortune with Silk Touch, by the kind of effect first; then consult Max Level and target eligibility. Benefit: avoids choosing by a high numeral while misunderstanding the function. Trade-off: this is not a best-build recommendation, and no compatibility or numerical yield is implied.

## Sources
EMI Enchanting 0.1.2 `assets/emi_enchanting/lang/en_us.json`; Enchantment Descriptions 21.1.11 `assets/enchdesc/lang/en_us.json`; processed EMI bindings. Retained claims E-ENCHANT-UI and E-ENCHANT-NAMED keep the exact language keys and archive hashes for independent review.
--- END PAGE: Read an enchantment before committing equipment ---

# Identify a target without breaking it

## Baseline
Jade 15.10.6+neoforge. How-to: use a world-target overlay as the starting point for item/recipe research.

## Evidence status
Exact shipped configuration descriptions and control labels; **not runtime tested**. The processed settings do not contain Jade's saved bindings, display mode or enabled information providers. No key such as a remembered default is prescribed.

## Prerequisites
A target you can safely look at and no need to mine or interact with it. An overlay is informational; it does not grant access to a container or establish that a block can be safely harvested.

## Steps
1. Look at the unfamiliar target without breaking it. If Jade's overlay is already visible, read the displayed name before acting. If not, find **Show Overlay** and **Open Config** in the controls/configuration UI via [control-context inspection](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/control-contexts/). Stop if you cannot identify their bindings.
2. Check **Display Mode** before treating Show Overlay as a toggle. The shipped descriptions distinguish **Toggle** (press Show Overlay), **Hold** (keep Show Overlay held) and **Lite** (hold Show Details for further information). The active mode is unknown here; follow the mode you actually observe rather than repeatedly tapping a key.
3. If the ordinary overlay gives too little detail, identify **Show Details** or **Show Details (Alternative)** and use the mode's instruction. Jade describes separate detailed/ordinary amounts for item storage and optional detail-only energy/fluid information. Their existence does not guarantee that every modded machine exposes those values.
4. Take the displayed name to [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/) to investigate ingredients and uses. Jade also ships **Show Recipes** and **Show Uses** action labels, but neither saved keys nor successful viewer integration are captured; use the established inventory-viewer route if those actions are unknown.
5. Before any actual harvest, distinguish name identification from tool/harvest information. A readable block name alone does not certify an appropriate tool, drop, ownership permission or safe machine removal. Leave the target intact if those conditions are unresolved.

## Expected result
A target name and, where supplied, additional information that can guide the next lookup without changing the block. This is the intended source-backed route, not an observed HUD result.

## Common failures
- **Overlay vanishes in a GUI, debug screen or player list:** Jade ships Hide from GUIs, Hide from Debug Screen and Hide from Player List options. Inspect the relevant option, not every rendering setting; saved values are unknown.
- **Only some information appears:** Lite mode and detail-only providers are possible documented causes. A missing field is not proof that a tank is empty or that a block has no stored energy.
- **Fluid target is hard to select:** Jade ships Toggle Fluid and a fallback option that shows fluid only when no other target exists. Their saved state is unknown; do not break a block to force a fluid target.
- **The HUD and boss bar overlap:** Jade includes boss-bar overlap choices including Hide Tooltip and Hide Boss Bar. This documents an option, not a recommendation to hide important combat warnings or a verified saved layout.
- **Name lookup finds the wrong item:** blocks and their item forms need not share every behavior. Recheck the target name and exact identifier when exposed; do not infer a recipe from a similar name.

## Tip: identify before interacting
Prerequisite: a working overlay on the target. Technique: read the name, then perform an inventory-viewer lookup before mining or opening an unfamiliar machine. Benefit: gathers an initial lead without changing the target. Trade-off: the overlay may be incomplete and is not a permission, break-safety or compatibility check.

## Sources
Jade 15.10.6 `assets/jade/lang/en_us.json`: key labels, display-mode descriptions, GUI/debug/player-list hiding, detail-only storage fields and fluid fallback. Claim E-JADE retains selected witnesses and exact archive identity. No HUD placement or live feature state was read or changed.
--- END PAGE: Identify a target without breaking it ---

# Start a character path

## Baseline
This is an bounded statically reviewed revision for candidate **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**, not an applied replacement for dev-0.5-20261015. New core text corresponds only to Tensura archive SHA-256 `2309a22ceb7e423e2f3212ea87686ccc510fe0003595500037e0b6c64baef213`. The original baseline/pins remain unchanged. Static descriptions and feedback are not runtime or award-handler proof.

Development snapshot dev-0.5-20261015: SoloCraft: Reawakening 1.3.3, Tensura: Reincarnated declared 2.0.1.3, and Tensura-Leveling 1.0.14 with its two co-packaged IDs.

## Evidence status

Bounded retained-evidence static review only; candidate dev-0.5-20261015-r2 remains unapplied. This is not runtime validation, integration approval, baseline adoption or publication.
Retained candidate-core translations and advancement objects now support named controls, race-choice information, learning feedback and requirement categories. The old hash failure remains historical; it no longer blocks reading the newly authorized retained text. First-join/screen-opening conditions, concrete per-race gates and the code emitting custom award triggers remain unresolved. Nothing here certifies a first awakening.

This is a route selector and safety guide, not a claim that your first awakening has been documented. It gives each system a separate starting state rather than treating both as a single leveling ladder.

## Prerequisites
A character already in the pack, access to your Controls menu, and a safe place to inspect menus. No command permissions, reset item, dungeon entry, or deliberate death is required for these inspection tasks.

## Steps

2. For Tensura, begin with [the built-in archive and evolution display](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/character-tools/) if you already have access to the character screen. This path now identifies the candidate core’s Status Menu control and a noncommitting inspection checkpoint. Initial access requirements remain unresolved by text resources.
3. Treat [the integration layer](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/character-integration/) as a separate explanation of explicit shipped declarations. Do not transfer a rank, skill award, resource amount, or gate from one system to the other merely because both are installed.
4. For a returning SoloCraft character with crystals already obtained, [craft the two documented items](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/solo-workbench/). This is a material-to-result path, not a promised source of starting crystals or hunter awakening.

## Expected result
You know which progression system owns your current task and which evidence supports its instructions. A menu inspection or craft is not proof of awakening, reincarnation, or cross-system award continuity.

## Common failures
- An advancement tree appears to demand another advancement first: a display parent is not an awarding gate. The selected SoloCraft awakening advancement uses an impossible trigger; its code-driven award path has not been traced.
- Advice says to die, reset, or spend a rare item to fix a menu: do not follow it from this chapter. No reversible reset or reliable recovery has been established for this snapshot.
- A helper screen offers a destination: a displayed branch is not proof that you can perform it now. Read its current requirement text; the full notes distinguish requirement categories from actual per-race gate proof.

# Inspect your SoloCraft character and quests

## Baseline
This is an bounded statically reviewed revision for candidate **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**, not an applied replacement for dev-0.5-20261015. New core text corresponds only to Tensura archive SHA-256 `2309a22ceb7e423e2f3212ea87686ccc510fe0003595500037e0b6c64baef213`. The original baseline/pins remain unchanged. Static descriptions and feedback are not runtime or award-handler proof.

SoloCraft: Reawakening 1.3.3 in dev-0.5-20261015. This is an independent SoloCraft entry route. A cross-system awakening requirement has not been established; no absence-of-gates claim is made.

## Evidence status

Bounded retained-evidence static review only; candidate dev-0.5-20261015-r2 remains unapplied. This is not runtime validation, integration approval, baseline adoption or publication.
Documented-static UI vocabulary, not runtime-tested screen navigation. The exact language file contains old panel labels and a reworked panel's labels together; presence does not prove that every control is visible in your active panel. Initial hunter-awakening eligibility, rank selection and first-join behavior have not been traced. In particular, the Hunter Rank Evaluator label is not proof of how to obtain or use it.

## Prerequisites
An existing character, a safe location, and access to Controls. If you have not received access to SoloCraft's panel, this guide can identify the control but cannot supply the missing award route.

## Steps
1. In Controls, find the category **Solo Leveling Keybinds** and the action **Open Panel**. Use your actual assigned key; this manuscript deliberately does not assert a physical default or overwrite a binding.
2. Use Open Panel in the game context. If no panel appears, stop at the control/access check in Common failures; do not enter a gate to force access.
3. If a panel is available, identify the displayed character values before changing anything. The selected language file names Level, Health, Mana, Fatigue, Strength, Vitality, Intelligence, Agility, Sense, Defense and Skill Points. Not all values necessarily appear in the reworked panel. Record only what you actually see.
4. If **Quests** is present, open it and inspect **Goals** and the current progress. The reworked panel's quest tooltip is Quests; older resources call the view Active Quests or Daily Quests. Use the actual label, not a screenshot from another release.
5. Complete only the goals your live quest displays. This source capture does not establish running distances, required kills, timer length or reward eligibility. Reopen the quest view and use its progress as your checkpoint; **QUEST REMAINS INCOMPLETE** is an explicit shipped status label, not a command to claim a reward anyway.
6. If **Collectible Rewards** is present, inspect the reward view after the quest reports completion. Do not assume an old reward-label quantity is your actual payout. The server can control quest rules, and no reward handler was disassembled in this lane.

## Expected result
You have identified the panel control and, where access exists, read your current state and quest goal. Successful opening and payout remain gameplay checks; this guide does not certify them from translations.

## Common failures
- Open Panel does nothing: check the named action's current binding and whether the game rather than another screen has focus. A key name exists, but the panel's entitlement condition is still unknown. Do not use a reset or administrator award as ordinary recovery.
- No daily quest: the shipped description for soloDailyQuest says the rule enables/disables system daily quests. Its live value was not read. Ask the host whether quests are enabled rather than changing a rule yourself.
- Conflicting penalty text: a language entry calls one quest optional while soloPunishment describes penalties for incomplete daily quests. Translation wording alone cannot decide active behavior. Treat the live timer and host's rules as unresolved until checked; no penalty-safe guarantee is given here.
- A job quest does not appear at a level from another guide: the selected rule description allows Job Change Quest Level 20–100, default 40. That is documentation of a configurable value, not the effective server threshold or a complete job-change unlock procedure.

## Sources

The awakening resource data/sololeveling/advancement/awakened.json uses minecraft:impossible, so the award handler must be traced separately. Claims CHAR-003 through CHAR-007.

# Craft documented SoloCraft equipment

## Baseline
This is an bounded statically reviewed revision for candidate **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**, not an applied replacement for dev-0.5-20261015. New core text corresponds only to Tensura archive SHA-256 `2309a22ceb7e423e2f3212ea87686ccc510fe0003595500037e0b6c64baef213`. The original baseline/pins remain unchanged. Static descriptions and feedback are not runtime or award-handler proof.

SoloCraft: Reawakening 1.3.3 in dev-0.5-20261015. How-to: turn already-owned materials into two exact shipped recipe results.

## Evidence status

Bounded retained-evidence static review only; candidate dev-0.5-20261015-r2 remains unapplied. This is not runtime validation, integration approval, baseline adoption or publication.
Documented-static shaped recipes, not runtime-tested crafting or effective pack recipe resolution. Crystal acquisition, rank entitlement, item use and any later progression award are outside these recipe objects. Neither recipe declares a hunter-rank condition; that does not prove the complete game lacks additional restrictions.

## Prerequisites
A crafting grid large enough for the shown pattern and the exact named ingredients. Check whether your current recipe viewer shows the selected result; return to [the independent SoloCraft route](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/solo-start/) for panel/access questions. A missing viewer entry is not alone proof the recipe is disabled.

## Steps
### Make the E Rank Sword
1. Obtain two **sololeveling:mana_crystal_e** and one **minecraft:stick** from your existing inventory. The source of the crystals is not documented here.
2. Place the two crystals above the stick in one vertical column: crystal, crystal, stick. The shipped pattern is a / a / b.
3. Inspect the result before taking it. The recipe declares **one sololeveling:e_tier_sword**, whose English name is **E Rank Sword**.
4. Take the result only if it matches. Using it successfully, its damage and any class restriction require separate evidence; the recipe does not award a rank.

### Make the Magic Reader
1. Gather four **minecraft:redstone**, four **minecraft:iron_ingot** and one **sololeveling:mana_crystal_d**.
2. Put redstone in the four corners, iron in the four edge-center slots, and the D crystal in the center. The pattern is aba / bcb / aba, with a=redstone, b=iron and c=the crystal.
3. Inspect the result: **one sololeveling:magic_reader**, named **Magic Reader** in the selected English translation.
4. Take the result if it matches. Do not equate this item with the separately named **Hunter Rank Evaluator** block. Crafting this item is not an evidenced awakening procedure.

## Expected result
One declared result per craft. The material totals and layouts come from the exact recipe objects. Successful crafting and use have not been observed in this documentation work.

## Common failures
- Empty output: first compare the ingredient IDs, pattern and grid size. If these match, stop and report the recipe ID plus what the viewer shows. Pack overrides and runtime recipe registration were not resolved by this lane.
- A different tier crystal looks similar: use the exact namespace and suffix, not only the icon or a generic crystal name.
- The crafted reader does not awaken you: no such effect is established. Do not repeatedly spend materials on the assumption that an item label promises a progression award.

## Sources
SLR1.3.3.jar: data/sololeveling/recipe/basic_sword_e.json and data/sololeveling/recipe/magic_reader_recipe.json; assets/sololeveling/lang/en_us.json item.sololeveling.e_tier_sword, item.sololeveling.magic_reader and block.sololeveling.hunter_rank_evaluator. Claims CHAR-008 and CHAR-009.

### Archive and internal-entry citations

These locators identify the documented archive contents, not downloadable retained evidence or a guarantee of effective gameplay.

- [CHAR-008] `SLR1.3.3.jar!data/sololeveling/recipe/basic_sword_e.json`: `$`.
