# Personal loot and the shared container

## Baseline

Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation. Static documentation only; effective datapacks, settings and gameplay have not been verified.

## Evidence status

This exact Lootr release describes unique loot for each player opening a loot container. Its shipped warnings explicitly say breaking the block or destroying the entity prevents other players obtaining loot. Personal contents therefore do not make the physical container disposable. Conversion eligibility, refresh configuration and claim permissions are not established by that description.

## Prerequisites

A container positively identified as Lootr, room for the items you intend to take, and host permission to interact. Ordinary base storage is not assumed personal. Do not probe by breaking a container.

## Steps

1. Agree that each player opens the intact Lootr container themselves. Do not nominate a first opener to distribute everyone's supposed contents.
2. Open normally and take the items you want into your inventory. Leave the physical container in place for other players.
3. A second player opens it independently. Their own contents are the intended checkpoint; different rolls need not be identical. This is a proposed group check, not a reported test.
4. Close it and return supplies to agreed shared storage yourself. Personal loot does not mean items subsequently placed in ordinary storage remain private.
5. Before moving on, read any displayed refresh or decay message. These messages describe timed states; their presence in translations does not mean either feature is enabled for every chest.

## Expected result

Players can distinguish their loot from the shared access point and preserve access for the group. Continue with [equipment slot reference](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/equipment-slots/).

## Common failures

- Cannot break: respect the refusal. Shipped messages mention sneak-breaking overrides in some contexts; they are destructive warnings, not a recovery instruction.
- Empty after returning: do not promise a refill or run refresh/clear commands. Timers and conversion can be configured; ask the host with the container's displayed identity, without publishing coordinates.
- Container decayed or destroyed: no player restoration procedure is documented in the retained evidence. Stop interacting and request host assessment; commands may change everybody's state.
- Claim denies access: Lootr ships an FTB Chunks whitelist tag reference, but that does not prove effective permission policy. Team/claim permissions require a separate host check.

Tip: open as a group before leaving rather than removing the chest. This preserves access but still cannot guarantee later availability if decay is enabled.

## Sources

- `lootr-neoforge-1.21.1-1.11.38.127.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`
- `lootr-neoforge-1.21.1-1.11.38.127.jar!assets/lootr/lang/en_us.json`
- `lootr-neoforge-1.21.1-1.11.38.127.jar!data/ftbchunks/tags/block/interact_whitelist.json`

# Research an already-owned relic

## Baseline

Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation. Static documentation only; effective datapacks, settings and gameplay have not been verified.

## Evidence status

The exact release's UI text supplies the research gesture, constellation task and wrong-container recovery. This is a research task, not a proven acquisition route or an executable numerical leveling build. Costs are dynamic UI substitutions, not fixed numbers recoverable from the translations.

## Prerequisites

An already-owned Relics item in the player's main inventory. Locate Controls → Research relic under the cursor. Have room to keep the item in inventory. Use [equipment slot reference](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/equipment-slots/) separately.

## Steps

1. Hover the relic in the main inventory and hold the currently bound research control shown by the tooltip. Do not assume a default keyboard letter.
2. In the research interface, choose an ability available at the item's current level. A low-level message means it is not ready; do not spend resources attempting to bypass it.
3. For an unresearched available ability, connect hidden stars so the constellation matches its background image. The shipped tip says correct alignment automatically researches the ability.
4. Check that the unresearched prompt is gone. Read the ability and Experience Sources tabs before using it. An experience source can itself require an associated ability to be unlocked and researched.
5. For later planning, distinguish relic experience from vanilla player experience and leveling points. The UI says relic level-ups increase the upgrade-point pool; ability upgrades also list player-XP costs. Read the current cost before any purchase. This guide does not direct spending or prescribe an untraced upgrade click.

## Expected result

An available ability is researched through the documented puzzle. This does not establish its equip requirement, combat effect or save persistence. Optional [Full-spoiler content omitted] gives a separate exact cooking upgrade.

## Common failures

- Wrong-container message: move the relic to the player's main inventory and retry; this is the release's explicit recovery.
- Hint offers a price: hints consume displayed player experience. Solve manually to avoid that expense; Shift offers automatic research, not a free shortcut.
- Quality mistaken for ability level: UI text calls quality a visual summary of random characteristics. A higher quality display is not itself more levels.
- Reroll/reset: do not use them as free undo. Both display player-XP costs, and rerolling can replace perfect values; Shift can automate costly repeated operations.

Tip: inspect Experience Sources before training. It identifies relevant use instead of wasting unrelated XP; exact quantities remain item- and state-dependent.

## Sources

- `relics-1.21.1-0.10.7.8.jar!assets/relics/lang/en_us.json`

# Encounter readiness

## Baseline

Bosses of Mass Destruction 1.3.3, Minecraft 1.21.1. Documentation baseline; not a gameplay acceptance result.

## Evidence status

This is a preparation and go/no-go task, not an encounter activation or combat tutorial. The shipped metadata describes high-threat mobs. Exact source-to-binary control correspondence and effective server settings are not established. Recommendations below are conservative planning choices, not mod-enforced requirements. [S1]

## Prerequisites

- A party decision about acceptable losses and who may initiate encounters.
- Ordinary replacement equipment, food and an agreed fallback location. No particular armor tier, weapon damage or number of supplies is guaranteed sufficient.
- Read [exploration preparation](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/exploration-preparation/) before leaving a shared base.

## Steps

1. Separate essential supplies from optional valuables. Leave irreplaceable belongings behind; take only a kit you can afford to lose. This limits loss rather than preventing death.
2. Agree on a single encounter initiator. Until exact controls are confirmed for the server, do not use unfamiliar items on suspicious blocks merely to discover their function. This also avoids accidental item consumption.
3. Before approaching an unfamiliar site, identify an ordinary retreat route and a fallback meeting place. Do not count an untested portal, teleport item or grave interaction as your way out.
4. Decide a retreat threshold before fighting: for example, inability to keep the agreed route clear. This is a party policy, not a supported boss-reset mechanic. A teammate leaving does not prove the encounter ends.
5. Assign one player to observe the result and one to keep the retreat clear. Record only what actually happened: an advancement notification, items seen and items collected are different checkpoints.
6. Choose reconnaissance instead of activation when the return route, initiator permission or replacement kit is missing. The optional [Full-spoiler content omitted] distinguish declared rewards from observed rewards and explain why an upstream activation recipe is not yet an exact installed-build guarantee.

## Expected result

You have a replaceable kit, one named initiator, a retreat decision and a return route that does not rely on an untested control. This completes readiness planning only; it does not establish a discovered structure, a summon, victory or successful recovery.

## Common failures

- **Someone already activated the encounter:** do not promise a reset or refund. Prioritize withdrawing by the known route; avoid spending more unfamiliar items while diagnosing it.
- **A player dies:** a rescue is a new risk decision, not an automatic step. Do not assume inventory retention, a grave, loot protection or shared advancement credit.
- **The route becomes unsafe:** abandon the attempt rather than proving the equipment was sufficient by repeated losses. Replaceable kits lower the cost of this choice, but cannot make combat safe.
- **The party returns with no reward:** compare observations in the full notes; a loot declaration alone is not proof that it ran.

## Sources

- [S1] `BOMD-NeoForge-1.21-1.3.3.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml` — version, mod identity and high-threat description. Kit/party recommendations are editorial precautions, not claims derived from a combat implementation.
--- END PAGE: Encounter readiness ---

# Craft an oak drawer

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
> Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Storage Drawers **13.11.4**. Static recipe task; effective recipes and gameplay are not tested.

## Prerequisites
Prepare one `storagedrawers:oak_full_drawers_1` from its shipped recipe, if that recipe is available in your current recipe view. This page covers crafting, not drawer placement, deposit, withdrawal or persistence. Use [the route comparison](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/storage-choice/) if you have not chosen between workshop storage and [a carried backpack](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/first-backpack-access/).

You need a 3-by-3 crafting grid, six `minecraft:oak_planks` and one ingredient that your current recipe view accepts for `c:chests/wooden`. Tag membership and resource overrides are not established by the declaration. Do not substitute another wood just because its texture is similar.

## Steps
1. Use [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/) for `storagedrawers:oak_full_drawers_1`. Compare the displayed output and ingredients with the declaration below before committing materials. A missing entry or different output is a boundary to resolve, not permission to change pack settings.
2. For the matching declared recipe, fill the top and bottom rows with oak planks. Put the accepted wooden-chest-tag ingredient in the centre of the middle row; leave its other two slots empty. The exact shaped rows are `["///", " X ", "///"]`, where `/` means oak plank and `X` means the chest ingredient.
3. Check that the output is one `storagedrawers:oak_full_drawers_1`, then take that single output. The shipped declaration specifies a yield of one, not multiple drawers.

## Expected result
The recipe-supported target is one oak full drawer item with one compartment. This is not evidence of its capacity, controls or successful storage. Keep bulk stock in its existing container until a separately supported deposit-and-withdrawal task is available; [storage boundaries](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/storage-boundaries/) explains what remains unproved.

## Common failures
If no matching output appears, leave the output untouched and compare the exact item ID and current accepted ingredients. A recipe-viewer omission alone does not prove a recipe cannot load. If the result conflicts, do not repeatedly consume ingredients to diagnose it. Keep the ingredients rather than editing configs or installing another mod.

## Tips and trade-offs
Use a small first craft rather than converting all your chests: it limits the materials committed while access and capacity remain unresolved. This does not promise that crafting can be undone. Do not add a Void Upgrade to solve capacity: the shipped description says “Destroys excess items.” That warning establishes deletion intent, not the precise runtime overflow algorithm.

## Sources
- `StorageDrawers-neoforge-1.21.1-13.11.4.jar!data/storagedrawers/recipe/oak_full_drawers_1.json` — complete shaped recipe declaration.
- `StorageDrawers-neoforge-1.21.1-13.11.4.jar!assets/storagedrawers/lang/en_us.json` — `item.storagedrawers.void_upgrade.desc`; label evidence only.

# Craft and open a first backpack

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
> Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Sophisticated Backpacks **3.26.9.2195**. Recipe declaration plus provisional same-version upstream control evidence; no effective-recipe or gameplay acceptance.

## Prerequisites
Prepare one basic Backpack and, conditionally, open its held-item menu without equipping it or moving stock. Choose [an oak drawer](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/craft-first-oak-drawer/) instead for a workshop crafting route; these are independent alternatives, not prerequisites for each other. [Storage choice](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/storage-choice/) retains the wider comparison.

You need a 3-by-3 crafting grid, four ingredients accepted by `c:strings`, four accepted by `c:leathers`, and one accepted by `c:chests/wooden`. The shipped Backpack recipe uses a custom `sophisticatedbackpacks:basic_backpack` type and a `sophisticatedcore:item_enabled` condition for `sophisticatedbackpacks:backpack`. Its presence in a JAR does not prove it is enabled or displayed correctly in this pack.

## Steps
1. In [recipe search](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/recipe-search/), compare the current recipe for `sophisticatedbackpacks:backpack` with the shipped rows `SLS`, `SCS`, `LLL`: S is the string tag, L the leather tag, C the wooden-chest tag. Confirm the output is one basic Backpack before committing ingredients. If the current recipe cannot be confirmed, retain your ingredients and resolve that uncertainty first.
2. For the matching declaration, arrange those three rows and take one `sophisticatedbackpacks:backpack`. Do not add upgrades or put valuables inside it during this first access check.
3. **Provisional control, not exact-build proof:** upstream source declaring `mod_version=3.26.9` opens the Backpack menu from the held item's `use` method. With the new Backpack selected in your main hand, aim into empty air and use the item (your configured Use Item control). Do not sneak-use it on a block: the same source has a separate placement route. This source has not been tied to build 2195 or the installed compiled behavior; if your interface differs, stop instead of trying guessed bindings.

## Expected result
For the provisional control in step 3, the expected result is a Backpack container menu, not equipping or placing the item. Read only its title and visible layout; leave slots and upgrade controls untouched. Menu opening is not proof of deposit/withdrawal, capacity, safe item eligibility or save/reopen persistence. A first useful carrying-and-return task remains unverified until those boundaries are established.

## Common failures
If the provisional use action does not open the expected menu, do not compensate with a Curios slot, drop action, repeated placement or forced pickup. Keep the empty Backpack and record which item and action differed. Missing recipe display can reflect conditions, custom serializer or viewer support; do not rewrite settings to force a recipe. Do not infer item loss merely from a menu failing to open.

## Tips and trade-offs
A held-item access check avoids making worn-slot eligibility a prerequisite, but does not establish worn upgrades or shortcut keys. Avoid deletion upgrades while learning: the shipped Void Upgrade tooltip says it voids items selected in its filter. The Filter Upgrade tooltip instead describes filtering items piped in/out. Those labels distinguish purposes, not proved controls, pipe compatibility or non-deleting capacity expansion. Keep [transfer and deletion boundaries](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/storage-boundaries/) open.

## Sources

- `sophisticatedbackpacks-1.21.1-3.26.9.2195.jar!assets/sophisticatedbackpacks/lang/en_us.json` — selected Filter/Void Upgrade labels, not UI proof.

# Plan shared-base access without changing permissions

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
**Development documentation snapshot:** Minecraft 1.21.1 / NeoForge 21.1.250; FTB Teams 2101.1.10 and FTB Chunks 2101.1.22. Exact-version upstream declarations and command registration support this preflight; effective permissions, compiled correspondence and multiplayer behavior are not runtime verified.

## Prerequisites

You need the base owner's agreement on who may build, open ordinary containers, use machines and invite others. This task produces an access request, not a claim or a permission change. No world, map or team screen needs to be opened to complete it.

## Steps

1. Make an access-request table with four columns: person/role, intended action, target type and owner decision. Separate placing/breaking blocks from interacting with a block. FTB Chunks declares separate `block_edit_mode` and `block_interact_mode` properties; do not collapse these into one promise of “base access.” [Property declarations](https://github.com/FTBTeam/FTB-Chunks/blob/a90e58e431879f5213629373ea9cde7001ebef45/common/src/main/java/dev/ftb/mods/ftbchunks/api/FTBChunksProperties.java#L36-L44).
2. Add separate rows for real players and automation using fake players. The source declares `allow_fake_players`, `allow_named_fake_players` and `allow_fake_players_by_id`. These are policy inputs, not evidence that a particular machine is authorized. Leave their owner decisions unresolved rather than enabling them. [Fake-player properties](https://github.com/FTBTeam/FTB-Chunks/blob/a90e58e431879f5213629373ea9cde7001ebef45/common/src/main/java/dev/ftb/mods/ftbchunks/api/FTBChunksProperties.java#L15-L20).
3. Record which owner will confirm membership. For a later owner-authorized session, `/ftbteams info` is a documented information command: its handler sends team-info lines and invokes the `TeamEvent.INFO` extension event. Its subscribers are untraced, so it is **not proven side-effect-free**; no harmful behavior is demonstrated. This is a source reference, **not permission to execute any command, join or start a world now**. Any later invocation requires separate owner authorization before action, with the untraced INFO event explicitly considered. Treat its output as private; do not publish identities. Do not substitute `/ftbteams info server_id`. [Registration](https://github.com/FTBTeam/FTB-Teams/blob/20bc18fd15c5c71f19be530e86fd0db038e2acd4/common/src/main/java/dev/ftb/mods/ftbteams/data/FTBTeamsCommands.java#L158-L165), [information handler](https://github.com/FTBTeam/FTB-Teams/blob/20bc18fd15c5c71f19be530e86fd0db038e2acd4/common/src/main/java/dev/ftb/mods/ftbteams/data/FTBTeamsCommands.java#L347-L351).
4. Keep ordinary shared containers separate from [personal-loot expectations](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/personal-loot/). Permission to interact is not proof that everyone sees the same loot, that a transfer succeeds, or that items persist. FTB's block-interaction policy can return `CHECK`, which explicitly means further checks are needed. It is not an allow/deny matrix for every modded container. [Interaction policy](https://github.com/FTBTeam/FTB-Chunks/blob/a90e58e431879f5213629373ea9cde7001ebef45/common/src/main/java/dev/ftb/mods/ftbchunks/api/Protection.java#L44-L56), [CHECK meaning](https://github.com/FTBTeam/FTB-Chunks/blob/a90e58e431879f5213629373ea9cde7001ebef45/common/src/main/java/dev/ftb/mods/ftbchunks/api/ProtectionPolicy.java#L3-L15).
5. Mark all proposed changes and tests as requiring the owner. A future disposable-container test should distinguish opening, depositing, withdrawing and persistence after reconnect; it must not use valuables or personal-loot containers as a proxy for ordinary storage. This is a safety test design, not a completed test or a claim about current access.

## Expected result

Every requested action has an owner decision or an explicit unknown. You have not changed membership, claims, chunk loading or settings. A completed table is planning progress; shared-base operation remains unverified.

## Common failures

If permission is denied later, stop the attempted operation and ask the owner to compare the exact action and target with the agreed table. Do not unclaim land, disband a team, invite everyone or broaden fake-player policy to make one container work. Preserve current membership while diagnosing. Unknown effective rules cannot be repaired from these source declarations alone.

## Tip and trade-off

Keep build access narrower than container access when the owner wants that distinction. The separate source properties make it a meaningful policy question, but their actual values and downstream checks still require owner verification. This reduces accidental permission expansion at the cost of a longer preflight.

## Next steps

Check [connection responsibilities](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/connection-preflight/), [backup recovery gates](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/backup-recovery-safety/) and [navigation control attribution](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/navigation-controls/) before a separately authorized shared session. Team membership, transport connectivity, claims and personal loot are different questions; success in one does not close the others.

## Sources
The inline exact-source citations and explicitly historical observations above support only their stated boundaries; safety recommendations are not observed gameplay.

# Check connection responsibilities before sharing a world

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
**Development documentation snapshot:** Minecraft 1.21.1 / NeoForge 21.1.250; e4mc 6.1.0. Version-matching upstream source supports a bounded preflight, not a successful connection, authenticated access policy or service-availability guarantee. The upstream build declares 6.1.0 but uses a 1.20.2 compile target; this is source-version correspondence, not proof of installed-binary equivalence on this pack. [Build declaration](https://github.com/vgskye/e4mc-minecraft-architectury/blob/765e93282765d0d23c1f2820e56b7c96e0f1bf71/gradle.properties#L3-L8).

## Prerequisites

Name a host owner and invited players privately. Agree on the same pack revision and a session window. This is an offline planning task: do not open a world, choose Open to LAN, reveal an address or join a server to complete it.

## Steps

1. Write down who runs the world and who merely connects. Upstream's usage instruction is “Open to LAN as normal.” Treat that as the **exposure boundary**, not a harmless way to inspect options. Do not invoke it during this preflight. [Usage](https://github.com/vgskye/e4mc-minecraft-architectury/blob/765e93282765d0d23c1f2820e56b7c96e0f1bf71/README.md#L17-L19).
2. Assign the host responsibility for the running world, saves and recovery approval. The source starts a relay session from the server TCP-listener hook when `hostEnabled` is true and stops its session from the server stop hook. A saved setting or installed mod does not establish that a relay is live. This is not unattended server rental, a promise of uptime or evidence that the external relay is available. [Listener lifecycle](https://github.com/vgskye/e4mc-minecraft-architectury/blob/765e93282765d0d23c1f2820e56b7c96e0f1bf71/common/src/main/java/link/e4mc/mixin/ServerConnectionListenerMixin.java#L37-L56).
3. Assign guests responsibility for matching the agreed gameplay pack. Do not infer from the host-side relay hook either that every guest needs e4mc or that every gameplay mod is optional on guests. Exact guest-side requirements and full-pack join/rejoin remain unverified here. Record that question before distributing a pack; do not install or remove anything based on this page.
4. Ask the owner for an explicit access-control decision before any later exposure. The selected source alters vanilla whitelist-command permission checks to allow the single-player owner, otherwise retaining the original predicate. That establishes a command-authorization hook, **not that a whitelist is enabled, populated or enforced for this session**. Do not issue whitelist or ban commands as an inspection shortcut. [Owner permission hook](https://github.com/vgskye/e4mc-minecraft-architectury/blob/765e93282765d0d23c1f2820e56b7c96e0f1bf71/common/src/main/java/link/e4mc/mixin/WhitelistCommandMixin.java#L15-L26).
5. Agree on private address handling, how the host will announce session closure, and who approves a later reconnect test. Keep addresses, account identities and connection output out of public support requests. Verify [shared-base policy](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/shared-base-preflight/) independently: transport access does not grant container or land permissions.

## Expected result

Your session note identifies host, guests, pack revision, access-control approver, private communication channel and closure/reconnect owner. Unresolved guest requirements and live access-control checks remain explicit. No connection has been opened or tested by this task.

## Common failures

If a later authorized session cannot connect, confirm that the owner intended the session to be live and that everyone used the agreed pack revision. Do not publish the address, disable security, forward ports or change team claims as speculative fixes. Escalate the specific failure privately. No stable-address/rejoin guarantee is established; an old address is not evidence of a current session.

## Tip and trade-off

Separate “can connect” from “can use the base” in your session checklist. This avoids widening [FTB permissions](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/shared-base-preflight/) to solve a transport failure, at the cost of checking two distinct boundaries. Before any future recovery, use the [stopped-copy checklist](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/backup-recovery-safety/); a guest's client installation is not an identified backup of the host's world.

# Prepare a stopped-copy backup recovery checklist

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
**Development documentation snapshot:** Minecraft 1.21.1 / NeoForge 21.1.250; SimpleBackups 1.21-4.0.21. The [selected release metadata](https://api.modrinth.com/v2/version/6JQL4qSR) links the [source commit](https://github.com/ChaoticTrials/SimpleBackups/commit/b3cc8b033f042471592031c6d69297e55e36ba67) used below. This is release-linked upstream evidence, not a fresh binary audit, effective configuration check or successful restore.

## Prerequisites

The world owner must identify the world, backup custodian and recovery objective privately. This task prepares a checklist only. Do not create, inspect, extract, restore or delete a backup; do not open worlds or modify settings while following it. The later stopped-copy work requires separate owner authorization and verified backup inputs.

## Understand what must be verified

- **Mode:** `FULL_BACKUPS`, `MODIFIED_SINCE_LAST` and `MODIFIED_SINCE_FULL` are distinct modes. The default declaration is full backups; that is not the effective setting. A partial archive is not a complete world. [Mode declarations](https://github.com/ChaoticTrials/SimpleBackups/blob/b3cc8b033f042471592031c6d69297e55e36ba67/src/main/java/de/melanx/simplebackups/config/CommonConfig.java#L46-L53).
- **Location and retention:** output path, per-world subdirectories, count and storage-size limits are configurable. Old files can be deleted by retention logic. Do not assume the default folder or an old timestamp proves a usable recovery point still exists. [Configuration](https://github.com/ChaoticTrials/SimpleBackups/blob/b3cc8b033f042471592031c6d69297e55e36ba67/src/main/java/de/melanx/simplebackups/config/CommonConfig.java#L54-L75), [retention implementation](https://github.com/ChaoticTrials/SimpleBackups/blob/b3cc8b033f042471592031c6d69297e55e36ba67/src/main/java/de/melanx/simplebackups/BackupThread.java#L117-L153).
- **Scope:** the implementation walks the selected world tree, skips `session.lock` and `biomancy.spatial.db`, and applies configured exclusions. It does not establish that launcher settings, the whole pack or every external mod database is backed up. [Backup traversal](https://github.com/ChaoticTrials/SimpleBackups/blob/b3cc8b033f042471592031c6d69297e55e36ba67/src/main/java/de/melanx/simplebackups/BackupThread.java#L219-L280).
- **Completion:** a ZIP filename or a started notification alone is insufficient. The source's finished notification occurs after backup creation and retention-size processing; completion still does not prove restore integrity. [Run lifecycle](https://github.com/ChaoticTrials/SimpleBackups/blob/b3cc8b033f042471592031c6d69297e55e36ba67/src/main/java/de/melanx/simplebackups/BackupThread.java#L156-L171).

## Steps

The following are **owner gates for a later authorized recovery**, not actions performed by this documentation task or a verified SimpleBackups restore command.

1. **Authorization gate:** record who owns the world, which recovery point they approve and where the separate disposable destination will be. Require explicit approval for access to private backup/world contents. Do not request public uploads.
2. **Stopped-state gate:** require the owner to save and stop the host/server normally and confirm it remains stopped before copy/restore work. If it cannot be confirmed, defer recovery. Do not overwrite an active world.
3. **Rollback gate:** require a separate preserved copy of the current stopped world and the original backup set before attempting recovery. Record identities privately. Preserve originals unchanged; a rolling backup folder subject to retention is not the only copy to rely on.
4. **Backup verification gate:** require the custodian to verify integrity, intended world, timestamp, actual backup mode and exclusions. For partial backups, require the necessary full base and dependent partial set plus a supported reconstruction procedure. That exact reconstruction procedure is **not established here**; do not extract only the newest partial ZIP and call it restored. A missing dependency blocks the attempt.
5. **Disposable-copy gate:** require the approved procedure to target a new, separate stopped-copy location only. Verify archive root layout and the compatible pack revision before later use. Do not merge into or replace the live world. Exact extraction/reconstruction commands are deliberately not supplied without the verified inputs and owner-approved procedure.
6. **Acceptance gate:** before any production switch, require separately authorized copy-only load, inventory/container checks, relevant dimensions, normal save/stop and save/reopen checks. Record the owner's acceptance and rollback destination. Neither authoring this checklist nor seeing a finished notification passes those checks.

## Expected result

The owner can say which gates are satisfied, who verifies each remaining gate and why recovery is blocked if any prerequisite is missing. The deliverable is a recovery decision checklist, **not a restored world**.

## Common failures

If integrity, backup mode, dependencies or target identity cannot be verified, keep both original world and backup set unchanged. Stop at the failed gate rather than trying alternate archive orders against the live world. Escalate to the custodian privately; do not “fix” a missing backup by deleting old files or changing retention.

## Tip and trade-off

Prefer a verified complete recovery set preserved outside the rolling retention destination before any later experiment. The cost is additional storage; the benefit is avoiding dependence on files the documented retention logic may remove. This is a safety recommendation, not a claim that any copy currently exists.

For a shared world, identify the [actual host owner](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/connection-preflight/) first and keep [base permissions](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/shared-base-preflight/) separate from backup custody.

## Sources
The inline exact-source citations and explicitly historical observations above support only their stated boundaries; safety recommendations are not observed gameplay.
--- END PAGE: Prepare a stopped-copy backup recovery checklist ---

# Identify navigation controls without opening a map

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
**Development documentation snapshot:** Minecraft 1.21.1 / NeoForge 21.1.250; Xaero's Minimap 26.6.0, Xaero's World Map 1.47.0 and FTB Chunks 2101.1.22. This reference uses previously processed saved bindings, not live controls or private map data. The saved-controls observation predates this documentation snapshot and has not been refreshed. It does not prove exact-version navigation behavior or current effective bindings.

## Prerequisites

You want to distinguish map providers or report an overlapping control without revealing terrain, waypoints or server identity. This task needs only the reference below and your intended action; do not launch a game or open a map to complete it. For a later authorized controls review, use [control contexts](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/control-contexts/), not trial keypresses over valuables.

## Read the saved-control reference

These are historical **saved values**, not factory defaults or instructions to press them. The literal action identifiers let you distinguish similarly named providers.

| Provider and action identifier | Previously saved value | Boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Xaero World Map `gui.xaero_open_map` | M | Same saved key as FTB map; do not invoke to diagnose overlap |
| FTB Chunks `ftbchunks.map` | M | A second provider, not evidence of synchronized maps |
| Xaero minimap settings `gui.xaero_minimap_settings` | Y | Saved settings control; no permission to change it |
| Xaero World Map settings `gui.xaero_open_settings` | Right bracket | Also saved for Sophisticated transfer-to-inventory; context matters |
| Xaero waypoint screen `gui.xaero_waypoints_key` | U | Also a recipe-viewer uses key; opening waypoints exposes private state |
| Xaero new waypoint `gui.xaero_new_waypoint` | B | Creation control; not an inspection shortcut |
| Xaero instant waypoint `gui.xaero_instant_waypoint` | Keypad add | Creation control; do not test as a harmless toggle |
| Xaero enlarge map `gui.xaero_enlarge_map` | Z | Also saved for Twilight zoom; not a proved conflict |
| Xaero World Map zoom `gui.xaero_map_zoom_in` / `gui.xaero_map_zoom_out` | Unbound / unbound | `key.keyboard.unknown` is the recorded value, not a key called Unknown |
| FTB minimap zoom `ftbchunks.minimap.zoomIn` / `ftbchunks.minimap.zoomOut` | Equals / minus | Do not transfer these keys to Xaero by analogy |

## Steps

1. Write the desired action and provider: for example, “identify the Xaero World Map control,” not just “map broken.” Use the table to separate the two map providers without opening either.
2. Classify the intended action as display, settings or state creation. Keep waypoint creation separate from viewing. Do not press B or Keypad add merely to discover what happens.
3. Record the screen context in which the problem happened, without coordinates, terrain screenshots or waypoint names. A saved duplicate key is a possible overlap, not proof that both handlers run in that context.
4. If a later controls-only inspection is authorized, compare the current action entry with this historical reference. Controlling 19.0.5 supplies Show All, Show Conflicts, Show Unbound and Available Keys. Its exact labels are documented in `Controlling-neoforge-1.21.1-19.0.5.jar!assets/controlling/lang/en_us.json` (`options.showAll`, `options.showConflicts`, `options.showNone`, `options.availableKeys`). Inspect only; this task prescribes no reset or remap. Missing/unbound entries do not establish missing map data.
5. Produce a short support note: provider, action identifier, intended context, historical binding and whether a current value is known. Leave current values unknown if they have not been inspected. Do not attach map folders, personal settings exports or server-profile files.

## Expected result

You can name the map provider and action without opening a private map, creating a waypoint or changing settings. This does not establish navigation, dimension switching, waypoint sharing, teleport permission, map persistence or restoration behavior. Those mechanics need additional exact-version evidence and separately authorized acceptance.

## Common failures

If M selects an unexpected provider later, do not delete map caches or reset both mods. Preserve personal map state and report the provider/context mismatch first. If U opens an unexpected screen, return to the original context rather than creating a waypoint to test the key. See [inventory safety](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/inventory-safety/) before any future item-context investigation.

## Tip and trade-off

Use action identifiers in a support note instead of a screenshot of your world. This makes two M bindings distinguishable without exposing explored terrain; the trade-off is that a control-only report cannot diagnose the map's contents. A map display also does not prove [claim permissions](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/shared-base-preflight/).

## Evidence limits

The binding table is a historical saved observation, not an archive-derived default table. Only the Controlling labels above have the cited exact-version archive-entry witness. Actual Xaero navigation controls, gestures and map/waypoint lifecycle remain unsupported by the available exact-version evidence; no navigation tutorial is claimed.

## Sources
The inline exact-source citations and explicitly historical observations above support only their stated boundaries; safety recommendations are not observed gameplay.

# Prepare a comfort and graphics choice

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
> Development documentation snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. Static records are not effective settings or runtime acceptance.

Choose what to discuss or change later without replacing the saved look. This is a paper/settings-choice task, not a tuning recipe. You need the pack revision and your own description of discomfort; no game session, config edit or diagnostic upload is required.

## Prerequisites
Use only the revision label and already-known observations described above. This task does not require opening the game or private files.

## Steps
1. Describe one symptom in plain language: camera jolts, movement softness, glare, text size, or black rendering. Note whether it is owner-observed or only a question. Stop playing if you feel unwell; a diagnostic is not worth worsening symptoms.
2. Use the saved observations below as a comparison, not instructions to copy. Write “unknown” for your effective value unless you already know it. Do not open personal configuration files to fill this worksheet.
3. Write the desired outcome and the features to preserve: **Distant Horizons, shaders plus meaningful frame generation, and motion comfort**. A suggestion to disable generation globally or while moving is a diagnostic trade-off, not completion of that objective. No such switch is requested here.
4. Pick one question for the pack owner: for example, “Can text be made easier to read without changing the shader preset?” Record the present state, proposed scope, approval needed and how the original choice would be restored. Do not apply a new preset or activate an optional addon.
5. Check the record: it names one symptom, distinguishes saved from observed state, preserves the objectives, and requests no automatic rewrite. If it cannot, narrow the question using the [support checklist](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/support-escalation/).

## Compare with saved observations
The [snapshot evidence definitions](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/sources/) distinguish selected saved keys from runtime behavior. These are historical saved observations, not universal accessibility defaults:

| Area | Recorded value | What not to infer |
|---|---|---|
| Camera | `bobView=false`, `autoJump=false`, `damageTiltStrength=0.0`, `fovEffectScale=0.0`, `screenEffectScale=0.0` | Not a promise that every mod camera effect is suppressed. |
| Other effects | `darknessEffectScale=1.0` | Screen-effect reduction does not establish darkness reduction. |
| Readability/audio | `guiScale=2`, `gamma=1.0`, `soundCategory_master=0.25` | Not a prescribed size, brightness or safe listening level for every person. |
| Steady Sight | `strength=OFF`, `smoothStepCamera=true`, `optimizeVanillaSettings=false`, `optimizeOtherMods=false` | OFF is the saved strength key, not proof that the entire mod is inactive. Keep the vignette off; do not enable automatic optimizers to inspect them. |
| Iris | `enableShaders=true`, `shaderPack=MakeUp-UltraFast-9.5g.zip` | Selected is not proof that the shader rendered in a session. |
| MakeUp override | `AA_TYPE=0`, `BLOOM=false`, `SHADOW_CASTING=false` | Do not silently restore shadows from older preferences or guess numeric enum meanings. |

The installed identities are Steady Sight 0.3.0, Iris 1.8.12 and FrameGen 1.3; identity does not prove their combined rendering or generation state. Exact identity citations: `steady_sight-0.3.0+neoforge-1.21.1.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`; `iris-neoforge-1.8.12+mc1.21.1.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`; `framegen-1.3-neoforge-1.21.1.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`. Metadata supports identity only, not the saved-key table.

## Common failures
If advice conflicts with these observations, keep both labelled: historical saved state and proposed choice. Ask the owner to resolve the conflict; do not overwrite either. If the exact menu label or reset behavior is unknown, stop before changing it. Black rendering is a support symptom, not proof of a particular mod's fault.

**Tip:** a written one-variable proposal is easier to approve and undo than “optimize everything.” Its benefit here is clarity, not a claimed FPS gain. This task ends with a usable choice record; it does not establish relief, performance or live uptake. Continue with [graphics and documentation roles](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/graphics-and-books/).

# Prepare a privacy-safe support request

## Baseline
Development documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**; not release validation.

## Evidence status
> Development documentation snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. Static records are not effective settings or runtime acceptance.

Prepare a text-only request before deciding whether any diagnostic collection is necessary. You need the revision label and an account of what you already observed. Do not reproduce a crash, start hosting, launch a world or run a profiler for this checklist.

## Prerequisites
Use only the revision label and already-known observations described above. This task does not require opening the game or private files.

## Steps
1. Name the documentation revision, Minecraft/loader versions and affected system. If your installation may differ, say so instead of asserting an exact match. Use [graphics roles](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/reference/graphics-and-books/) to separate renderer, shader, distant terrain and frame-generation questions.
2. Describe the action, expected result, actual result and whether it happens every time. State “not measured” for performance when appropriate. Separate camera movement, walking, hand animation and distant-terrain symptoms rather than grouping them as “lag.” Do not invent a reproduction you have not tried.
3. Add only already-known context: single-player versus multiplayer, host versus joining client, affected screen, and whether the symptom followed an approved change. Do not include the address, seed, coordinates, world name or another player's identity.
4. Review the request before sending. Remove account names/UUIDs, tokens, IP/domain addresses, local user paths, chat, maps/waypoints and world locations. A screenshot can expose these in overlays, chat and window titles: use text instead unless a reviewed crop is genuinely needed.
5. Ask the pack maintainer which **specific minimal excerpt** is needed and why, who will receive it, and whether it leaves your machine. Start privately with the maintainer; do not paste into a public issue or upload service by default. This page does not send anything.

## Copy this template
```text
Documentation revision / actual installation match: known or unknown
System and symptom:
Action already performed:
Expected / actual:
Frequency and last approved change:
Single-player or multiplayer / host or client (no address):
Already-known shader / DH / generation state, otherwise unknown:
Evidence level: owner observation / saved record / not measured
Requested next decision:
Redaction reviewed: yes or no
```

## Gate additional evidence
A complete log, crash report, profiler recording, F3+L archive, screenshot or world backup is not automatically safe to share. Review contents and upload/export behavior before authorizing collection. Treat F3+L bundles as private world-derived data, not just timing CSVs. This is a conservative sharing rule, not a claim that every bundle contains every sensitive field.

Spark is present as a diagnostic utility (`spark-1.10.124-neoforge.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`); that establishes neither a safe command nor an upload policy. Do not run commands, attach recording tools or generate a viewer link from this guide. Exact selected-build collection controls, output contents and service behavior need separate review and explicit permission.

**Checkpoint:** you have a redacted draft that separates facts from unknowns and asks one answerable question. No collection or upload is required to complete it.

**Recovery:** if a draft contains sensitive data, do not send it; remove the data and review again. If already shared, contact the recipient/platform to request removal and tell the owner what category was exposed without reposting the secret. Redaction is not retroactive access control.

**Tip:** report “loaded,” “saved enabled,” “visually accepted” and “measured improvement” separately. None implies the next. See [existing troubleshooting](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/troubleshooting/) for bounded saved-state explanations, and [comfort choice](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/comfort-settings-choice/) before proposing graphics changes.

## Expected result
A labelled choice record or redacted support draft as described above, not a settings change, diagnosis or runtime result.

## Common failures
Missing observations remain unknown. Stop before collection, changes or sending; retain the permission and privacy gates above.

## Sources
The inline exact-source citations and explicitly historical observations above support only their stated boundaries; safety recommendations are not observed gameplay.
--- END PAGE: Prepare a privacy-safe support request ---

# Find the right graphics or documentation system

> Development documentation snapshot: **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**. Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. Static records are not effective settings or runtime acceptance.

Use this reference to route a question, not as an install list. Archive metadata establishes recorded presence and identity only. Saved state, shipped documentation, effective behavior and runtime acceptance remain separate.

## Graphics and diagnostics roles
| System | Question to route here | Boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Sodium 0.6.13; Sodium Extra 0.6.0; Reese's Sodium Options 1.8.3 | Renderer/video-options questions | No exact menu path, compatible option combination or gain is established here. |

| Distant Horizons 3.3.3 | Distant-terrain/LOD questions | Keep DH; distant rendering does not imply distant machine simulation. Saved generation choices are not a tuning prescription. |
| FrameGen 1.3 | Generated presentation and hand/movement symptoms | Loaded does not prove generated output, animation correctness or reduced latency. Preserve shaders plus generation as the objective. |
| Steady Sight 0.3.0 | Comfort settings questions | Use the [choice worksheet](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/comfort-settings-choice/); do not turn on automatic rewrites. |
| Spark 1.10.124 | Possible diagnostics after explicit review | A profiler is not an FPS booster; use the [privacy gate](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/support-escalation/). |

## Books are not the same as libraries
Patchouli, Modonomicon and GuideME are recorded inventory entries awaiting framework-role verification. The retained identity records do not establish their documentation roles or independent progression systems. Modonomicon has archive filename/release label **1.120.7**, while the retained manifest reports **Implementation-Version 1.110.0** (`modonomicon-1.21.1-neoforge-1.120.7.jar!META-INF/MANIFEST.MF#Implementation-Version`). This unresolved discrepancy establishes neither a wrong installed build nor an effective runtime version; neither token is normalized. Their presence does not prove that a particular book exists, how to obtain it, or which key opens it. Check the content system's guide instead of crafting a guessed generic “guidebook.”

- For AE2, the [starter outline](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ae2-starter/) identifies shipped guide content; it is not proof of a powered storage network.
- For Ars, the [first spell guide](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/ars-first-spell/) distinguishes its documentation item from the spellcasting book. Do not consume your only documentation copy on an assumption that all books are interchangeable.
- For Occultism and Nature's Aura, use their [first workbench](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/occultism-first-workbench/) and [first garden](https://faizsaifulnizam.github.io/minecraft-pack-wiki/guides/natures-aura-first-garden/) pages, retaining their stated geometry and runtime limits.

Inventory identity citations: `Patchouli-1.21.1-93-NEOFORGE.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`; `modonomicon-1.21.1-neoforge-1.120.7.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`; `guideme-21.1.19.jar!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`. These citations establish identities, not book-opening mechanics. The linked content guides carry their own mechanic-specific evidence. This reference does not assign every shipped book to a framework.

Dependency libraries supply implementation support; they do not automatically need player crafting tutorials. Do not remove a library because it has no player-facing menu. Library necessity for a specific error requires an exact dependency/error trace, not a filename guess. Likewise a resource path containing “book” can be a model or registry rather than guide prose.

## Disabled means no enabled route
CIT Resewn and More Culling are recorded **disabled**, not suggested fixes or available active controls. Their metadata locators are `citresewn-neoforge-1.21.1-0.jar.disabled!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml` and `moreculling-neoforge-1.21.1-1.0.8.jar.disabled!META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml`. Do not enable them from this page. Entity Culling is a different recorded active archive; its presence does not change More Culling's disabled status.

**Lookup checkpoint:** you can name the subsystem and whether your statement is inventory, saved state or observed behavior. If you cannot identify an exact control, report that gap instead of trying a similarly named switch.

# Sophisticated Backpacks reference

## Applicability and evidence
Candidate documentation snapshot **dev-0.5-20261015-r2**, Minecraft **1.21.1**, NeoForge **21.1.250**. Candidate unapplied; not canonical adoption or release validation. Retained static documentation only; **not-runtime-tested**. No family completeness claim.

## What the retained reference supports
`sophisticatedbackpacks:backpack` declares rows `SLS`, `SCS`, `LLL`: S is `c:strings`, L is `c:leathers`, C is `c:chests/wooden`. Its custom `sophisticatedbackpacks:basic_backpack` recipe carries a `sophisticatedcore:item_enabled` condition. Presence is not proof that the effective recipe is enabled.[1]

The linked beginner route's held-item menu action is provisional: upstream source declares `mod_version=3.26.9` but is not tied to installed build 2195 or compiled behavior. Menu opening does not prove slot eligibility, insert/remove safety or useful carrying-and-return storage. Do not infer Curios controls or use a worn slot to compensate.[2][3]

Filter/Void Upgrade labels distinguish piped-item filtering from filtered deletion intent, not proved controls, compatibility or capacity. Avoid deletion upgrades and leave menu slots untouched during the provisional first-access check.[4]

## Missing tasks
- Beginner: build2195 source/binary correspondence; container/helper/slot predicates; safe deposit, retrieval and return.
- Advanced: capacities, upgrade transitions/content retention, filters, shortcuts and safe worn-slot removal.
- Integration: backpack-to-ME direction, priorities, filtering and overflow remain unknown.
- Runtime: effective recipe, successful opening, recovery, death and save/reopen persistence remain unconfirmed.

Stop if the provisional interface differs. Keep the empty Backpack; do not try forced pickup, dropping, repeated placement or valuables to diagnose access.[2]
